Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 551: So What If I Risk My Life?
---------------------------------------
Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Unit were engaged in a bloody battle at the final gate of the Blood Road Maze Formation.

“Watch the left!”

Jin Hagun swung his sword and blocked the hidden weapon flying toward him. From behind, a long spear shot out swiftly, grazed past Jin Hagun’s ear, then withdrew again.

All manner of mechanisms flew in from every direction, targeting the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit. As time passed, the mechanisms attacked with ever greater strength and speed.

“Hurry!”

Jin Hagun’s shout was directed at Jeong Gyeong.

Amid this asura-like chaos, Jeong Gyeong, the Demon Extermination Unit’s formation specialist, was striving to find the life gate of the trial. This gate had to be solved by using the Five Elements’ cycles of mutual generation and mutual restraint.

Stepping stones inscribed with the characters of the Five Elements lay across the floor.

First, it had to be solved using the cycle of mutual generation, and then the cycle of mutual restraint.

“Wood drains the energy of earth, and earth blocks the flow of water.”

Jeong Gyeong stepped in order from Wood (木) to Earth (土), then to Water (水).

The problem was that the formation did not allow this easily.

“Get down!”

At Jin Hagun’s shout, the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit all dropped flat at once.

Whirrrrrrrrr!

An enormous rotating blade passed over their heads at terrifying speed.

Truly, it was clear why this maze formation bore the name “Blood Road.”

“Fire (火) melts metal (金), and metal cuts wood.”

If he was even a little late in stepping onto the floor, he would fall into a death gate. Then they would have to risk their lives to escape again.

‘Done!’

Just as Jeong Gyeong threw himself forward to step on the final stone—

Shwaaaaaak!

A blade aimed for that very moment flew toward Jeong Gyeong. In his joy and excitement, he had been too hasty.

‘No!’

It was too late to dodge, and despair seized him. Even if his neck was cut off, he only hoped his body would continue forward and step on that stone!

Kaaang!

The rotating blade brushed past him. Jin Hagun had rushed in and struck the blade away. There was no time even to exchange thanks.

Jin Hagun charged back in the opposite direction, and Jeong Gyeong threw himself toward the final stepping stone.

After circling through the Five Elements, the instant he stepped on the final Wood (木) stone—

With a clank, every mechanism stopped. One warrior of the Demon Extermination Unit stared at a blade that had barely halted right before his eyes.

The mechanisms that had filled the space with clanging metal from every side began returning to their original places.

Srrrng.

A door hidden in the wall opened. At last, they had opened the life gate of the final trial.

Everyone looked around at one another and exchanged glances. None of them knew how many times they had truly crossed the threshold of death.

“You worked hard.”

Jin Hagun praised Jeong Gyeong.

“Not at all. If it weren’t for you, Unit Leader, we truly would have lost many comrades.”

All the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit nodded in agreement. Jin Hagun’s performance in this formation had been that outstanding. It was an opportunity for them to see him anew.

“Let’s get out for now.”

They had not yet killed Cheongoe, who had deployed the formation. Since the formation had been broken, once they passed through that door, he would show himself.

Just as they were about to enter the door, Cheongoe’s voice rang out.

“To break through all the way to the final gate without a single sacrifice… truly impressive.”

His voice echoed, making it impossible to tell where it came from.

“The Demon Extermination Unit. You are indeed worthy of being called the pride of the Martial Alliance.”

Jin Hagun felt an ominous chill. Even though they had broken through the final gate, there was leisure in the bastard’s voice.

“But it would be a shame to let you go just like that, so I prepared a parting gift.”

Clank. Clank. Clank. Clank. Clank.

More than ten doors opened from every direction. Beyond them were long corridors, and at the end of each corridor, people were bound. Some places held one person, while others held several.

“Please save me!”

“Please, help us!”

“Please, at least save my child!”

Dozens of blades were aimed above their heads. They trembled in terror, not knowing when those blades might fall.

Cheongoe’s voice sounded again.

“It won’t be easy to save them.”

Jin Hagun could tell. Each corridor where those people were trapped had dangerous mechanisms hidden within it.

Srrrrrrk.

In front of the life gate ahead, something rose from the floor. It was the scale that showed the time limit, the same as they had seen at the previous gate.

“If you fail to leave through that life gate within the time limit, you will have to break through the Blood Road Maze Formation from the beginning again—only this time, it will be even stronger. Horrifying, isn’t it? Perhaps not all of you will make it this far alive as you did now.”

If the maze had grown stronger, there would be many casualties. Even getting here like this had been far from easy.

“Everyone you fail to save will die. Now then, how many can you save? Who will be chosen by the proud Demon Extermination Unit?”

It was a statement that meant everyone else would die. Cheongoe was behaving like a true devil.

“You will live with guilt for the rest of your lives!”

Along with Cheongoe’s laughter, the scale began to operate, and time started flowing.

Judging by the speed at which the scale tilted, there was not much time.

Inside the corridors, everyone shouted for them to save them.

“There are eight people in that room. We should save the places with the most people first.”

“No. There’s a child in this room. We should save this side first!”

The warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit each reported the situation in the rooms.

Their gazes turned toward Jin Hagun. Deciding whom to save was an incredibly difficult choice. By his order, someone would live, and someone else would die. Even as this happened, the scale continued tilting.

Then Jin Hagun spoke quietly.

“There’s no need to agonize over whom to save.”

At Jin Hagun’s words, everyone looked at him in surprise.

Jin Hagun strode forward and closed, with his own hands, the door of the life gate through which they were meant to escape.

Boom.

After closing the door, Jin Hagun turned around and said to his subordinates,

“We save them all, and then we start this formation over from the beginning!”

“!”

Because it was a decision they had not expected, everyone looked shocked.

Jin Hagun went from room to room and shouted to them,

“We will save all of you, so please wait calmly!”

At that, sighs of relief came from each corridor.

Jin Hagun spoke again to his subordinates of the Demon Extermination Unit.

“Someone may call this decision foolish. Some of us may die. Perhaps all of us may die. But I believe this choice is what makes us different from the Demonic Cult and the evil sects.”

A look of apology crossed Jin Hagun’s face. He respectfully cupped his fist and bowed his head to his subordinates.

“I am sorry for not giving you the chance to choose. I will make my apology in the underworld.”

The warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit looked at one another once.

Clack, clack, clack, clack, clack, clack.

They all drew their swords at once and shouted,

“Exterminate demons and shatter evil! The path of chivalrous righteousness!”

From the very beginning, they had no intention whatsoever of defying the decision of their leader, Jin Hagun. If the leader of the Demon Extermination Unit told them to die, then dying was what the Demon Extermination Unit did.

Just as they were about to enter the first room to save the people—

Sssssss.

Everything around them began to vanish. The open doors, and the people who had been waiting desperately.

Now they were in a different plaza.

Incense burned in every direction, and the walls and floor were filled with all manner of incomprehensible characters.

At the center sat Cheongoe.

The look in Cheongoe’s eyes as he stared at Jin Hagun was filled with disbelief.

“You truly chose to close the life gate with your own hands?”

Blood streamed from his mouth.

The instant they saw that, Jin Hagun and the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit understood. The formation had been broken. And as the formation was destroyed, Cheongoe had suffered severe internal injuries.

Closing the door of the life gate with one’s own hands.

Astonishingly, that choice had been the method to completely destroy this Blood Road Maze Formation.

The room with a child, the room with many people to save—all those various situations had been traps. They were meant to make them agonize over whom to save, so they would fail to see the essence.

“You truly stayed in this maze formation to save people you don’t even know?”

To that question full of doubt, Jin Hagun answered calmly.

“It may be surprising to you, but to us, it is only natural.”

Cheongoe did not believe him. Looking at him as though he were a hypocrite, he asked again,

“Leader of the Demon Extermination Unit, were you always this kind of person?”

Jin Hagun could not answer that question. He found himself thinking that he did not really know either. Had he truly been this kind of person? Or had something changed him?

“Someone once told me, ‘You will become a magnificent Alliance Leader.’ So I am trying to become one.”

Jin Hagun drew his sword. Then the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit also drew their swords in unison and aimed them at Cheongoe.

Whooooo.

Though no wind blew, the sound of wind arose. Light burst forth from the bizarre patterns and characters on the floor.

Shiiik!

Jin Hagun released sword qi. But as if he were cutting through an illusion, the sword qi passed right through Cheongoe.

At that moment, Hwang Ju, who was skilled in sorcery, shouted,

“You must enter the circle he is sitting in and cut him directly!”

Indeed, Cheongoe was seated at the center of a circle filled with incomprehensible spells.

Just as Jin Hagun was about to launch himself toward him—

Swaaaaaaaaaa.

A terrible evil energy swept in like a storm.

Cheongoe had drawn up the last of his internal energy to unleash his secret sorcery.

Heart Demon Black Shadow Art!

In that instant, the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit flinched. Some spat curses, and some screamed.

Illusions appeared before all of them. They were the people and events hidden deepest within their hearts. Things they all wished to conceal.

“Everyone, recite the verses of the Righteous Heart Art!”

It was a mind cultivation art unique to the Martial Alliance, one that protected the heart from wicked sorcery.

As they recited the Righteous Heart Art, the illusions in their hearts vanished and reappeared over and over again.

When the Demon Extermination Unit resisted with the Righteous Heart Art, other patterns and characters around Cheongoe began to glow. This time, they shone in many brilliant colors.

Srrrrrrk.

Then shadows holding swords rose from the floor.

Hwang Ju shouted again,

“They are shadow ghosts! Extinguish the candles on all sides!”

The Demon Extermination warriors swung their swords as one and faced the shadow ghosts. When their blades collided with the shadow swords the ghosts swung, the sound of metal rang out.

Clang, clang, clang, clang!

Those beings might have been illusions, but the swords they held were real.

Even as the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit fought the shadow ghosts, they recited the Righteous Heart Art and struggled to extinguish the candles. The shadow ghosts attacked them desperately, trying by any means to stop them.

As battles erupted in every direction, Jin Hagun was advancing toward Cheongoe.

Shwaaaaaaaaaa.

An immense evil energy battered him.

His skin was tearing, and it was so difficult that he could barely breathe, but Jin Hagun recited the Righteous Heart Art and silently moved forward.

The shadow ghosts that tried to attack him were blocked desperately by the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit.

The closer he drew to Cheongoe, the more illusions appeared, shaking his heart. The illusion of his grandfather disturbed his mind.

—I do not regard you as my successor! Do you think you are qualified to inherit my position?

His grandfather glared at him with terrifying eyes. It was such a vivid illusion that his heart ached with resentment.

Jin Hagun recited the Righteous Heart Art and continued forward. The next person to appear was his younger sister.

—Brother, you are a hypocrite. All this effort to uphold chivalrous righteousness is ultimately for the purpose of becoming the Martial Alliance Leader, isn’t it? Why do you think your chivalry has become exhausting? It is because of this impure purpose.

He knew this too was an illusion created by sorcery, but it hurt his heart.

Clang, clang, clang!

The sound of swords clashing rang by his ears. He thought that if the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit had not been blocking for him, he would have been bewitched by these illusions and allowed those attacks to land.

This time, the figure of the child he had once saved appeared again.

—Even if it meant giving up the position of Alliance Leader, would you still save me?

Then, immediately, an image arose of people pointing fingers at him after he failed to become the successor.

—Just how incompetent must he have been, for his grandfather to be the Alliance Leader and yet still not inherit the position?

And what hurt his heart most in all these illusions was this:

‘Is the desire to become the Martial Alliance Leader all there is in my heart?’

That must be why it was burrowing into that feeling and attacking him. That fact disappointed him in himself. The fact that his desire to become Alliance Leader lay in the deepest part of him.

Like a money-grubber who had sold his soul for wealth, like a lecher who had sold his soul to lust—had he sold his soul to power?

He knew that making him feel this way was what the illusion was aiming for. Even knowing that, he was tormented. No, because he knew, he was tormented all the more.

Hiiiiiii.

The closer he drew to Cheongoe, the more the wicked energy reached its peak. His head swam and dizziness overtook him. He wanted to simply sink to the ground and give up on everything.

If there was a Jin Hagun suffering like that, then in another corner of his heart—

‘Don’t make me laugh! I’ll never lose to some sorcery like this!’

There was also a Jin Hagun gritting his teeth.

That Jin Hagun kept thinking of one person. He did not know why that person was Geom Mugeuk, but in this extreme moment, he needed him.

—I’ll overcome it too, just like you.

Then Geom Mugeuk’s answer seemed to ring out. If it were him, he would say something like this at a time like this.

—I told you not to try too hard. Trust only as much as an arm’s length, and do only as well as an arm’s length.

Thinking of Geom Mugeuk, Jin Hagun gritted his teeth and stepped forward.

He walked and walked. And at the moment he took another step, prepared even for his body to be torn apart—

The terrible demonic energy pressing down on him vanished all at once, as if it had been a lie. The distracting thoughts tormenting his mind vanished with it.

And before his eyes, Cheongoe was vomiting blood in great heaves. His internal injuries were too deep; there was no turning back now.

Jin Hagun had overcome Cheongoe’s Heart Demon Black Shadow Art by his will alone.

Cheongoe looked up at him venomously.

“You’ve staked your life on becoming Alliance Leader, haven’t you? Your chivalrous righteousness is nothing but a tool to become Alliance Leader.”

Even at the moment of death, he sought to leave a wound on his opponent. Jin Hagun said nothing to that malice.

Shiiiiik!

Thuk!

Jin Hagun’s sword pierced Cheongoe’s heart.

Without pulling out his sword, Jin Hagun spoke without looking back at his subordinates.

“What he said may be true.”

He honestly told his subordinates what he had not said to the man.

A brief silence flowed.

Then someone behind him spoke.

“What’s wrong with staking your life on it?”

When Jin Hagun turned his head to look back, it was Jeong Gyeong. He thought Jeong Gyeong was saying such a thing because Jin Hagun had saved him several times while breaking through the formation.

Hwang Ju took up his words.

“I would rather you did. I hope that desire comes before chivalrous righteousness or goodwill.”

Was it because of what he had shown within the formation?

Even at the words that he wanted the position of Alliance Leader more than chivalry, the warriors of the Demon-Exterminating Squad showed no particular agitation. Rather, some even said this.

“I don’t think ambition is bad either. I want to accomplish a great cause with someone like that.”

Perhaps because they thought it was the first chance they had ever had, even those taciturn men who seemed unlikely to speak each said a word.

“You have done so well until now. How could you do any better?”

“I think anything beyond that would be greed.”

“If you are too perfect, it makes things difficult for us.”

Jin Hagun had truly never imagined his subordinates would say such things to him. He had not known they held such thoughts.

Come to think of it, we have never once spoken openly and honestly. Even though we have shared life and death like this.

After silently gazing at his subordinates for a moment, Jin Hagun turned back to Cheongoe.

Cheongoe’s wide-open eyes had completely lost their light.

“Yes. I have staked my life on becoming Alliance Leader.”

Jin Hagun pulled out the sword embedded in Cheongoe’s heart.

Spurrrt.

Jin Hagun did not avoid the blood that sprayed over his body.

As the lifeless Cheongoe slowly collapsed sideways, the surroundings began to change.

Thud.

The place where Cheongoe fell was the courtyard of Cheonhwaru.

And so, under everyone’s watchful eyes, Jin Hagun and the Demon-Exterminating Squad returned.

Chapter 552: A Pitch-Black Hole in My Heart Too.
------------------------------------------------
Beneath the blazing sunlight, the Dark King was wavering perilously, as if she might vanish at any moment.

But Geom Mugeuk knew. She was not an opponent who would simply disappear into nothingness like this.

“It cost an enormous sum to save that man. What a shame.”

Even in this situation, she maintained her composure.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Geugak Soma and said,

“I should have blown off only half his head and sent him back alive. So they’d go bankrupt trying to save him.”

As he joked, Geom Mugeuk’s hand naturally touched Geugak Soma’s back.

Wooooong.

Internal energy began to flow stealthily into Geugak Soma. He was trying to share a portion of what remained of his internal energy with him.

Geugak Soma did not refuse and accepted the energy. The Dark King might target him now that his internal energy had run out. If so, Geom Mugeuk would try to protect him.

If that energy was going to be spent that way, it was better to pass it to him instead. As always, he trusted the Young Cult Leader’s choice.

“We wouldn’t have saved him twice. Even that one time, our opinions were divided.”

“Which side were you on? Ah, of course you must have been on the side that wanted to save him.”

The wavering shadow shook her head. Though she had come with the Fighting King, it seemed she had hoped he would not be saved.

“You were the one who rescued him, weren’t you?”

“I was only following orders.”

It was likely not a lie. To her, who lived in the world of night, all those who lived in the day would hold no meaning.

Was the one who gave the order Hwa Mugi? Or someone else?

Geom Mugeuk wanted to ask directly about Hwa Mugi. What was he doing now? Was he still the leader of these people at this point in time? What was he thinking?

But he had no intention of mentioning Hwa Mugi at all. Many things had already changed in this life, but even so, he would not touch that matter to the end.

Because he hoped that Hwa Mugi, at least, would appear at that future time according to his original fate. Because the ones who should face him were not the present them, but the future them.

Instead of asking about him, he asked about the Dark King.

“If you disappear from here, what happens to your true body? Does it die in the palanquin?”

Geom Mugeuk thought her real body must be inside the palanquin.

“Why do you assume I will lose to the Young Cult Leader?”

“Because we have no intention of losing.”

“The Demon Venerable’s internal energy must be exhausted, and you, Young Cult Leader, should have little internal energy left to maintain this Space-Time Transposition Art, no?”

Indeed, the sunlight that had been shining fiercely was gradually losing its brilliance. Unable to maintain the blazing sun, he was now allowing time in this place to flow.

“To be honest, even this much is truly astonishing. No matter if you are the successor of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, how did you gather such internal energy at your age?”

Geom Mugeuk truly displayed profound power that seemed without end.

“I am the incarnation of greed. So I had thought this much would be enough, but from now on, I intend to gather much more. I made up my mind after meeting you. When it comes to internal energy and martial arts, I must not be the incarnation of greed, but greed itself. Only then will I be able to face you people.”

In the meantime, all the internal energy had been transmitted to Geugak Soma. Even while conversing, Geom Mugeuk had displayed the divine feat of passing on internal energy without his opponent noticing.

Geom Mugeuk drew the Black Demon Sword. As though it had been tempered by the Fighting King’s fist, an even sharper edge of killing intent flowed along the blade.

“Now, let’s finish this. I have a banquet appointment with my friends.”

Geom Mugeuk did not lose his composure.

Geugak Soma, too, gazed at the Dark King with calm eyes, both arms hanging at his sides.

The Dark King’s gaze turned toward the two of them, then toward the sun behind them. In that brief time, it had descended quite a bit more.

The moment her eyes grew intense, black smoke whirled around her and rose into the air.

Where the smoke vanished, a woman stood. Astonishingly, the Dark King had been here, not in the palanquin.

“It won’t be a banquet. It will be the memorial day for all of you.”

A woman dressed in black martial robes.

Her appearance was the complete opposite of her voice. Her features protruded, were torn and unbalanced; she had, in truth, looked far more beautiful when she had been wavering.

Her voice was different as well. A grating, shrill voice that jarred the ears.

“You damned son of a bitch Young Cult Leader!”

As if this were her original personality, her two eyes were filled only with deep hatred.

“I think I understand why you descended into that person’s body to fight.”

Killing intent flashed through the Dark King’s eyes. She had expected mockery that it was because of her appearance, but Geom Mugeuk was looking at something else.

“You are someone with a strong desire to live.”

She did not deny it. When she descended into someone, even if that vessel died, she herself did not. But now that her body was revealed, it was different. She could truly die.

“Even if I die, I won’t die in this filthy world of yours!”

The Dark King launched a surprise attack, striking out with both palms.

Shwaaaaaak.

She thought Geom Mugeuk would dodge, but Geugak Soma, who had no internal energy, would not be able to.

Yet Geugak Soma evaded her attack and even counterattacked.

Shuk!

She barely avoided the Blood Calamity Finger that flew at her.

She had been the one to launch the surprise attack, yet she was the one more startled. The Dark King shouted irritably,

“You monstrous bastards! Where the hell does your internal energy come from?”

Shwaaaaaaak!

The ground overturned beneath her palm technique. The cliff they stood on shook as if it might collapse at any moment.

But the martial arts she displayed in a place that was not darkness were nothing more and nothing less than “impressive.” Merely impressive was not enough to catch Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma.

Compensating for her lacking skill with internal energy, she continued the fierce battle.

And from the very beginning of this fight, the moment she had been waiting for arrived.

As Geom Mugeuk approached her, he suddenly looked at his hand. A reddish hue was seeping into it. In the distance, the sunset had begun to fall—the hour of dog and wolf, when day and night crossed.

And this moment was the hour of the wolf.

For an instant, it seemed a white rim formed once more around her pupils.

Then her body changed back into a shadow of darkness.

Srrrk.

In that instant, she vanished from sight.

Geom Mugeuk was startled. He had never seen movement this fast before.

“She seems to have used the Dark Path Return Art!”

He had thought she would not be able to use it here!

Geom Mugeuk turned toward Geugak Soma, then flinched in shock.

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes sank coldly. She had not used the Dark Path Return Art.

The Dark King’s face was wavering over Geugak Soma’s mask.

The Dark King had descended into Geugak Soma’s body.

From the moment the fight began, she had aimed for only this single instant. The instant when the light disappeared and she could use the Dark Descent Art.

It would have been better if she could have descended into Geom Mugeuk’s body, but she could not descend into the body of Geom Mugeuk, who had trained in the Nine Flowers Demon Art and the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art.

Geom Mugeuk stared at Geugak Soma in shock. Geugak Soma’s eyes were also startled. It meant that though his body had been controlled, his mind had not. Even with the Fighting King, the two had remained separate.

“One cannot use the Dark Descent Art twice in a single day.”

Her voice had once again become alluring and beautiful.

“If one does, a curse descends. What curse might that be?”

She continued with shocking words.

“It is the curse of never being able to escape the body one has descended into. I will be with Geugak Soma forever.”

“Don’t talk nonsense!”

Geom Mugeuk did not believe her. If such a curse existed, she would never have used the descent art.

Her clear, ringing laughter echoed out. She truly laughed like a madwoman.

“That’s right, it is nonsense. I simply cannot leave this body for the next three days. Until then, we live together and die together. That is more than enough time to kill all of you.”

Geom Mugeuk looked into Geugak Soma’s eyes beneath her wavering gaze. Rather, the eyes of the one directly involved were calm.

But his body was being controlled by the Dark King.

Shuk!

Blood Calamity Finger shot from Geugak Soma’s finger. His hand and the wavering shadow of the Dark King overlapped as they moved. Geugak Soma could not control his own body. That was because the internal energy of the descended Dark King was greater.

Geom Mugeuk threw himself aside and avoided the Blood Calamity Finger. He had never imagined a situation would come where he had to dodge Geugak Soma’s Blood Calamity Finger.

Shuk! Shuk!

Geom Mugeuk threw himself aside again. The Blood Calamity Finger grazed past his side.

“No!”

Despite Geugak Soma’s cry, the Blood Calamity Finger instead split apart and aimed for Geom Mugeuk.

Pak! Pak!

Blood burst from Geom Mugeuk’s shoulder. Once the Dark King descended into Geugak Soma, the power of the Blood Calamity Finger became even faster and stronger. For Geom Mugeuk, whose internal energy was exhausted, it was nothing short of a calamity.

“Do not worry, Lord Soma! I will find a way!”

Even while dodging the Blood Calamity Finger, Geom Mugeuk instead reassured Geugak Soma. But no method came to mind.

He could not release the Space-Time Transposition Art.

He could not know the situation outside, and even if Jin Hagun and Bisain were waiting for him, he could not attack Geugak Soma.

What worried him more than anything was the possibility that she would disappear with Geugak Soma using the Dark Path Return Art.

If Geugak Soma were dragged away by them like this?

They would kill Geugak Soma miserably. And if they kept him alive, his fate would be more wretched than death.

Puh-uk!

The Blood Calamity Finger bent and split apart, spraying blood from Geom Mugeuk’s body once more.

Geugak Soma shouted,

“Release the Space-Time Transposition Art, Young Cult Leader!”

He meant for him to join forces with the comrades outside.

‘At this rate, the Young Cult Leader will die.’

But Geom Mugeuk did not release it.

Geugak Soma thought there was only one way to resolve this situation.

“Young Cult Leader, you must kill me.”

The Dark King laughed and said,

“I know how much the Young Cult Leader cherishes you. The Young Cult Leader will never be able to kill you.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded and admitted it.

“I would rather die myself than do that. Please wait. I will find a way!”

Of course, Geugak Soma believed in Geom Mugeuk.

But right now, there was no time and no internal energy.

At this rate, he would die here. The killing intent of the Dark King lodged in his body was firm, and Geom Mugeuk would never attack him.

Geugak Soma’s eyes deepened as he resolved himself to something.

Shuk shuk shuk!

Puk! Puk!

Because he was using his remaining internal energy to maintain the Space-Time Transposition Art, Geom Mugeuk continued to be wounded. He avoided fatal injuries, but Geom Mugeuk’s entire body was soaked in blood.

Whiriririk.

Black smoke rose from beneath Geom Mugeuk’s feet like vines and bound his body. Injured and exhausted, Geom Mugeuk could not shake off that energy. Even in this situation, Geom Mugeuk did not release the Space-Time Transposition Art.

“Now, I’ll finish you with the very move that blew off that man’s head.”

The moment she raised her hand to unleash the Demon Extreme Berserk Palm.

The Dark King’s eyes widened in shock.

“!”

Only her wavering arm had stretched forward.

Geugak Soma’s arm remained where it was. His body had rejected her.

‘He can withstand the command of descent? How?’

It was something the Dark King had never experienced even once.

The Dark King tried to control him again and make him move. But Geugak Soma did not budge. Red veins burst in the two eyes within the mask.

And that was not all.

Geugak Soma’s arm began to rise slowly. He was not merely rejecting the Dark King; he was moving on his own.

Geugak Soma’s mind was filled entirely with white. He was now standing in his room. Standing there, staring at the long line drawn across the wall. The power of darkness could not invade the mind of Geugak Soma, who stood in the midst of pure white.

The place Geugak Soma’s hand was headed was not toward Geom Mugeuk. It was rising toward his own head. Since she had said she could not leave this body for three days, he intended to die together with the Dark King.

“No! Lord Soma! You mustn’t!”

Despite Geom Mugeuk’s cry, Geugak Soma’s arm continued to rise.

“Lord Soma! Soma, no!”

Though Geom Mugeuk shouted, the arm kept rising.

The Dark King was desperate as well. If Geugak Soma died, she too would die.

Black energy flowed out and tried to stop him, but it could not overcome Geugak Soma’s will.

The power Geugak Soma displayed now surpassed human will.

“Release this! Hurry!”

Without hesitation, the Dark King released the energy binding Geom Mugeuk. First, she had to survive.

The very instant the palm was heading toward Geugak Soma’s white mask—

Grip.

Geom Mugeuk seized his arm and turned the palm toward himself. By a hair’s breadth, the Demon Extreme Berserk Palm was not unleashed.

Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma’s gazes tangled in midair. The two asked and answered with their eyes.

Are you truly going to do this?

Yes. If it means I can save the Young Cult Master, I will.

Yes, he would. Just as I will not give up on him, he will not give up on me. That is why I have to find another way.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to the Dark King’s wavering shadow.

‘If only I could grab that!’

Geom Mugeuk seized that arm.

But it slipped through his hand like smoke.

He grabbed and grabbed again, but the shadow could not be caught.

‘Please! Please!’

When that desperation reached its peak, Geom Mugeuk’s body responded.

Wooooong!

With a deep vibration, something that had only been sleeping within his body until now awakened.

The energy that Bigwe had absorbed began to move. Among it, the energy now stirring was none other than the Black Crystal’s power.

As the Black Crystal’s energy rushed into Geom Mugeuk’s hands, black energy began to ripple around them as well.

Clench.

The next moment, the Dark King’s wrist was caught in Geom Mugeuk’s hand.

The Dark King was shocked, and so was Geom Mugeuk. The Dark King’s astonished expression rippled over the white mask. Beneath it, within the eyeholes, Geugak Soma’s eyes also shone with surprise.

“I caught you!”

Geom Mugeuk gripped the Dark King’s hand tightly. The Dark King tried to shake him off, but how could Geom Mugeuk ever let go of that hand? He pulled with the resolve that he would not release it even if his arm were severed.

Riiiiip.

Her body began to be dragged out of Geugak Soma.

“Lord Soma! Back!”

Geugak Soma exerted force in the opposite direction, trying to free himself from her.

“……Impossible!”

The dark energy flowing from her shocked body clawed at Geom Mugeuk’s arm.

Pak! Pak!

Blood sprayed from his arm, but Geom Mugeuk did not let go of her hand. Fortunately, the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Thread wrapped around his arm prevented it from being cut.

Geom Mugeuk had only one thought: if he let go of this hand, Geugak Soma would die.

“Just a little more! Just a little more! Uaaaaaaah!”

Riiiiip.

Little by little, she was pulled out.

And the very moment the Dark King was completely separated from Geugak Soma’s body—

Hwaaaaak! Sarak!

The Black Crystal’s energy devoured that darkness in an instant, as if swallowing it whole.

“Aaaaaagh!”

At the same time, her original form was revealed.

“You! What in the world are you!”

Geom Mugeuk was just as shocked as she was. Her dark energy had already been absorbed by the Black Crystal’s power, and then, as if nothing had happened, it lay dormant within his body.

“……No!”

Her face, having completely lost its dark energy, turned pitch-black in an instant.

Psssss.

She became black ash and scattered into the air.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been staring blankly at the sight, collapsed onto his back as if falling.

Geugak Soma also lay down where he was.

“Haa, haa.”

Only the ragged breathing of the two echoed through that place.

When this fight had begun, neither of them had ever imagined it would end like this.

Once their breathing settled, Geugak Soma asked,

“How did that happen?”

“There’s a pitch-black hole in my heart too. I suppose it ate her.”

It was half true, but Geugak Soma took it as a joke.

“Is your body all right?”

“No. It hurts so much I could die.”

Geugak Soma was the first to sit up.

Blood was flowing down Geom Mugeuk’s arm. He remembered the moment that arm had pulled him free to save him. If not for the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Thread, it would have been cut off.

Without a word, Geugak Soma pressed the acupoints on his arm to stop the bleeding. Geom Mugeuk exaggerated his pain and complained.

Now it was Geom Mugeuk’s turn to ask.

“Are you all right, Lord Soma?”

Geugak Soma’s gaze as he looked at Geom Mugeuk deepened. He felt that the moment had come to do what he had resolved to do for quite some time.

“Whether I am all right…… please see for yourself.”

Geugak Soma slowly began to remove his mask.

Chapter 553: If You're Late, You Become the Youngest.
-----------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk suddenly recalled that day.

The moment he had finished two days of training with Geugak Soma within the Time-Space Transposition Art.

At the end of that bloody battle, Geom Mugeuk had changed the place they were in into a snowy landscape out of consideration for Geugak Soma.

—Next time, you must travel with me.

—When that time comes…

Back then, Geugak Soma had hesitated. Geom Mugeuk had wondered what he wanted to say that he would hesitate so uncharacteristically.

Now he felt he knew.

That hesitation had meant he would remove his mask.

Geom Mugeuk did not stop him.

Even in his life before the regression, he had shown his face, and in this life as well, he had taken off his mask of his own accord.

It seemed it was fate that they were to live seeing each other’s faces.

Srrrk.

Geugak Soma’s hand trembled slightly as he removed the mask.

The face of Geugak Soma revealed beneath the mask.

When Geom Mugeuk had seen his face before the regression, it had been after far more years had passed. It had been during the days when they went to search for the final material for the great rite.

He had been handsome then, too, but now Soma was much younger than he had been then.

Geugak Soma was truly handsome. If the Poison King’s handsomeness was that of a beautiful young man, and the Drunken Demon’s was a handsomeness born of his unique air, then Geugak Soma’s was perfect handsomeness.

His features were in perfect harmony, and his gaze was clear yet deep.

Who in the world could look at this face and think of Geugak Soma?

“How did you endure it? Holding back the desire to boast of such a handsome face.”

Geugak Soma laughed. Until now, Geom Mugeuk had only ever seen his eyes laugh, but now he saw his entire face smiling.

“I took it off because I wanted to drink comfortably with the Young Cult Leader. Drinking beneath a mask has no savor to it.”

He knew that was not the reason. Geugak Soma was showing him the strongest trust he could offer.

Geugak Soma silently looked down at the mask. The mask was smiling back at him.

What could he be thinking right now?

That much he could not know, but at least one thing was certain.

The moment Geugak Soma removed his mask, he had become even stronger.

Until now, the mask had protected him. The mask was his symbol, his identity, and his pride. The mask had made him strong, and the mask had made him wicked.

“From now on, I will wear it when I want to wear it, and take it off when I want to take it off.”

Now he had become free from the mask.

Sssssss.

Just then, their surroundings began to change. As his internal energy ran completely dry, the Time-Space Transposition Art began to disappear.

“Do you remember? I said that if you took off your mask, I would treat you to an incredible meal.”

Geugak Soma nodded.

“When we return to our cult, I will treat you to good wine and food.”

Putting his mask back on, Geugak Soma said,

“I will look forward to that day.”

* * *

Until Han Seol arrived here, she had never imagined she would experience something like this.

How could she have imagined that the moment she arrived at the meeting place with Geom Mugeuk, she would end up freezing the dark energy flying toward the young alliance leader of the Evil Dao Alliance?

How could she have known she would see blood-soaked martial artists breaking through a formation?

—Seol, this is no ordinary matter. How about we withdraw for now?

How grave must the situation have been for the Ice Twin Swords guarding her to send such a voice transmission?

‘Young Cult Leader, just what sort of gathering did you invite me to?’

It truly seemed just like him. Of course, she had no intention of withdrawing at all.

Bi Sain ran over to Jin Hagun, who had just emerged from the formation, and asked,

“Are you all right?”

Jin Hagun turned back and checked on the Demon-Extermination Squad warriors first. Fortunately, every single one of them had safely escaped the formation.

Only then did Jin Hagun nod and answer,

“We’re all right.”

“That’s a relief.”

At that moment, the two men were feeling a similar emotion.

‘To think the day would come when I would be this glad to see this man.’

When Jin Hagun had turned back a moment ago, Bi Sain had seen the looks they exchanged. Those gazes had become even more tightly bound than before they entered the formation.

Just as they had, those men too had fought a desperate battle in which they crossed the threshold of death.

“And you?”

“It wasn’t easy on this side either, but we settled it well. Lady Han was a great help.”

Bi Sain introduced Han Seol to Jin Hagun.

“This is the young palace mistress of the Northern Sea Ice Palace. And this friend here is the commander of the Martial Alliance’s Demon-Extermination Squad.”

At the words that he was the commander of the Demon-Extermination Squad, Han Seol was inwardly surprised. How could she not know that he was a leading successor candidate for the Martial Alliance leader?

So the friends the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had said he would introduce her to were none other than the successors of the Martial Alliance and the Evil Dao Alliance.

She had long known that Geom Mugeuk was no ordinary person, but to think he kept company with them as friends.

“Brother!”

Just then, Jin Haryeong and the mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion also emerged from the secret room. When the sounds of battle outside ceased, Jin Haryeong had cautiously come out to check, and realized that the fighting was over.

Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong were relieved together to see that each other was safe.

Jin Haryeong looked around and asked,

“The Young Cult Leader?”

At Jin Haryeong’s question, Jin Hagun looked at Bi Sain. Bi Sain said nothing and merely looked at the palanquin. It meant he was still fighting.

Jin Hagun answered Jin Haryeong.

“He’ll be on his way.”

The exact same words Bi Sain had said earlier flowed from his mouth. The measure of the two men’s faith was similar.

Thus, everyone spread out a perimeter around the palanquin.

On one side stood Bi Sain and the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao; opposite them stood Jin Hagun and the Demon-Extermination Squad; on another side stood the Faceless Guests and Jin Haryeong; and opposite them stood Han Seol and the Ice Twin Swords.

How much time passed like that?

Wuuung.

The palanquin began to tremble.

The moment everyone stepped back, drew their weapons, and tensed.

Light burst out from the palanquin. The light streaming from every gap in the palanquin spread through the darkness and stretched upward as though it would touch the sky.

That intense light vanished in an instant with a whoosh.

Sssssss.

The palanquin began to disappear as it turned to ash.

As everyone watched in shock,

“Where are you looking? We’re over here.”

They turned around to find two people standing side by side. They were Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma, both covered head to toe in blood.

Just by looking at their battered appearances, one could tell what kind of battle they had fought.

Geom Mugeuk quickly looked around first. Everyone who should have been there was there.

Relief spread across Geom Mugeuk’s face.

It was an emotion that could neither be faked nor disguised.

Only then did Geom Mugeuk collapse onto the ground and joke,

“Aigo! My arms, my legs. My whole body hurts as if I’ve been beaten all over. No, wait, I actually was beaten all over.”

Only then did everyone’s faces relax, and smiles appeared. Because this joking was the signal that the battle was over.

Still sitting on the ground, Geom Mugeuk asked Han Seol,

“How do you find the Central Plains?”

To invite a person and then greet her covered in blood like that? Even so, seeing him safe, she was inwardly glad and happy.

“I don’t recall you telling me this banquet would be a bloody banquet.”

It was a rare joke from her, who was not given to joking.

“A proper festival ought to have some fun surprises like this, should it not?”

When his greeting with her ended, Jin Hagun and Bi Sain approached him.

Geom Mugeuk looked more haggard than he ever had in all the times they had seen him. They could tell he had truly used up every last bit of strength and returned.

“Now is the perfect chance to kill the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s joke, Bi Sain and Jin Hagun looked at each other. When their eyes exchanged a look that said, Shall we really try it? Geom Mugeuk quickly said,

“I’m joking. I only made that joke because I trusted the person behind me!”

A smile formed on Bi Sain’s rugged face.

“In that case, it really is an opportunity.”

When Geom Mugeuk turned around, Geugak Soma was no longer there.

At some point, he had gone over to Yeojeong and was speaking with her.

“No, you abandon me the moment we come out!”

Even at Geom Mugeuk’s shout, Geugak Soma pretended not to hear.

Seeing Yeojeong smiling brightly in front of Geugak Soma, Geom Mugeuk felt anew that they had truly returned safely.

Yeojeong looked at Geom Mugeuk and quietly bowed her head. He knew without her saying what kind of greeting of the heart it was.

Bi Sain and Jin Hagun, who had been standing over him, also squatted down beside Geom Mugeuk.

Because they were two men who seemed least likely to do such a thing, the sight of the two successors squatting on the ground with Geom Mugeuk between them was a scene one would hardly see in a lifetime.

Geom Mugeuk expressed his gratitude to Bi Sain.

“If you had not cut off the internal energy, I would have died.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Bi Sain asked in surprise,

“How did you know I cut off the internal energy?”

“When the lines connected to the palanquin were severed, yet you still didn’t come to help me, I guessed it. I fought while looking only toward the moment when you would cut off that boundless internal energy.”

Bi Sain was happy to know that his efforts had not been in vain.

Originally, Bi Sain had meant to tease Geom Mugeuk by asking what he would have done if it had not been for him, but now that the situation had actually come, such jokes did not come easily. He felt anew that jokes, too, had to be made by someone accustomed to making them.

Like Geom Mugeuk right now. And his target was none other than Jin Hagun.

“You’re feeling regret right now, aren’t you? If this friend had entered the formation and you had been outside, you would have been the one to save my life.”

Jin Hagun shook his head.

“If that had been the case, we would have lost a friend.”

“You mean Sain wouldn’t have gotten out of Cheongoe’s formation? Are you looking down on our young alliance leader right now?”

“No. I mean I wouldn’t have thought to cut it off.”

It was an honest answer that Geom Mugeuk would have died because of that, and also words that held Bi Sain in high regard. Jin Hagun had once valued face and pride greatly, but in his eyes now was the composure seen only in someone who had let go.

Because he had let go, both of his hands were empty. What, indeed, would he take hold of with those hands now?

“You two mustn’t keep becoming so impressive! Being impressive is my role.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Jin Hagun and Bi Sain supported him from either side and helped him stand.

This time, Geom Mugeuk thanked Jin Haryeong.

“Thank you for keeping your promise.”

“I only hid in the secret room.”

“That’s why I’m even more grateful.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze toward her deepened.

“Tell me honestly. How many times did you want to rush out?”

Jin Haryeong smiled. This was why she liked Geom Mugeuk. Because he was someone who understood her heart without her having to say it. Because he understood that hiding in the secret room had been harder than coming out and fighting.

“About ten times?”

“Fewer than me.”

“What was?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to Bi Sain. She had wondered how he would let it pass.

“The number of times I begged because of this slowpoke. Please cut off the internal energy already! I begged while getting beaten half to death.”

“Don’t exaggerate! If you’d been beaten that badly, your opponent would have been reduced to dust.”

“Huh! How did you know? He really did turn to dust and scatter.”

Only then did the three of them look at one another and laugh.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward one place.

“Ah, over there!”

Everyone turned their heads and looked where Geom Mugeuk was staring.

Far off on the horizon, light was spreading and pushing back the darkness.

Within the Time-Space Transposition Art, the sun had been setting, but outside, in reality, the harrowing night had passed and morning was dawning.

Everyone silently watched the sunrise, more magnificent than ever before.

The mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion stepped forward and spoke in a bright voice.

“Thanks to all of you, everyone in our pavilion has been saved. I sincerely thank you.”

The courtesans, cooks, and workers who had been hiding nearby all came over and bowed their heads with words of gratitude.

“Please, everyone, rest. We will prepare this evening’s banquet with all our hearts.”

Thus, another battle that had begun with one courtesan’s escape came to an end.

* * *

Returning to his room, Geom Mugeuk replenished his internal energy through circulating and regulating his qi.

He had truly used it all up so completely that he wondered when he had ever drained his internal energy to the very bottom like this before.

He circulated his true qi once, then twice, then three times. He repeated it until his internal energy was fully replenished.

After filling his internal energy, Geom Mugeuk took out the secret coffer and placed it on the table.

Looking at the smiling secret coffer, he expressed his gratitude.

“If not for you, I might have been in serious trouble. Thank you.”

But as if nothing had ever happened, the black crystal energy that had swallowed the Dark King’s energy, and the other energy as well, lay dormant deep within his body as though they had never existed in the first place.

“Just what are you?”

The secret coffer merely smiled. Yes, someday, the day would come when he would find out.

He put the secret coffer back into his robe and lay down on the bed to sleep. Circulating his qi and regulating his breath relieved his fatigue to some extent, but what rest was better for a person than sleep? Geom Mugeuk fell asleep the moment he lay down.

In his dream, he walked through darkness.

It was not the loathsome darkness of the Dark King. It was the darkness that had accompanied him in his life before his regression.

There were good things about darkness, too. It could hide loneliness. Wasn’t one lonelier at night? Not necessarily. At times, the loneliness bestowed by a bright world was far more powerful.

How long had he walked through that darkness?

At the end of it, he met a presence.

Was it because his seventh-stage Nine Transformations Demonic Art had failed several times? Or was it because his subconscious had wanted to see him?

The being waiting for him was the Heavenly Demon Soul.

Geom Mugeuk silently looked up at the enormous Heavenly Demon Soul. Today, the outline of its face was a little clearer than the last time he had seen it. Why? Could this fight with the Dark King have brought him some achievement in the Nine Transformations Demonic Art?

—Did your pride get hurt, by any chance?

Even when he spoke to it like this, the Heavenly Demon Soul always merely looked down at him with haughty eyes. Today, however, its reaction was different.

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s head nodded ever so slightly.

—Are you listening to me?

Geom Mugeuk woke from his sleep shouting those words. The last words had actually come out like sleep talk.

“…It definitely nodded.”

Though it had been a dream, Geom Mugeuk’s heart pounded at the Heavenly Demon Soul’s reaction.

* * *

He thought he had only slept for a short while, but when he went outside, it had already become evening.

Cheonhwaru was in the midst of preparing for the banquet. The walls were shattered and holes gaped here and there, but rather than detracting from it, they lent tonight’s banquet a certain charm.

The chefs and workers of Cheonhwaru all came out in full force to prepare the food. They cooked and made ready for the banquet with the heart of serving the benefactor who had saved their lives.

“We’ll be drinking all night tonight! Please prepare plenty of liquor!”

The one who responded to Geom Mugeuk’s words was not the master of Cheonhwaru, but Jin Hagun, who had arrived ahead of him.

“You haven’t seen the liquor storehouse here, have you? There’s no need to worry about that.”

Come to think of it, Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad had disguised themselves as workers at Cheonhwaru, carrying liquor and working.

Geom Mugeuk sat across from Jin Hagun and said,

“Ah! An alliance leader with experience working in a brothel, no less! How am I supposed to deal with you from now on?”

“I don’t know, but I’m sure you’ve done even worse.”

“Of course I have. I’ve even played the role of a servant.”

At those words, Jin Hagun laughed. Sitting face-to-face with Geom Mugeuk like this felt comfortable now. Perhaps people really did grow alike when they spent time together, because he found himself making jokes like this.

“Have a cup of the liquor I carried in, servant!”

“You’ll have to carry it many times tonight, elder!”

As the two tilted their cups, Jin Haryeong and Han Seol arrived.

Bi Sain was someone who was usually never late to an appointment, yet for some reason, he was late today.

“All his brothers are gathered here, so why is the youngest so late?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Jin Hagun said,

“Young Alliance Leader Bi is probably much older than you.”

“If you’re late, you become the youngest. That’s how it works.”

Just then, Geom Mugeuk looked toward the entrance and gave an inscrutable smile. The others all looked over as well, their eyes showing surprise.

Bi Sain was entering, neatly dressed in white robes he did not usually wear.

Chapter 554: Since we fought together, we should drink together.
----------------------------------------------------------------
All eyes were focused on Bisain.

Since Bisain, who usually went about in black robes, had donned white robes for the first time, gazes were naturally drawn to him.

As he walked over, Bisain desperately avoided Geom Mugeuk's eyes.

He had expected to be teased about his attire as soon as he sat down.

Geom Mugeuk filled his wine cup and spoke.

“My savior, have a drink.”

Bisain looked at Geom Mugeuk with suspicious eyes.

‘I can't let my guard down!’

It was Geom Mugeuk, after all—who knew when and how he might fluster him.

Without revealing such inner thoughts, Bisain expressed his gratitude as well.

“Thanks to you, I protected my reputation.”

Originally, those bastards had tried to use Bisain to eliminate the Lady of Thousand Flower Tower. If Geom Mugeuk hadn't intervened, their fates would have become far more entangled.

Just then, the Lady of Thousand Flower Tower, bringing the dishes herself, spoke with a bright smile.

“Not to mention, you saved all the lives of Thousand Flower Tower.”

She set the food on the table. Today's spread was truly a feast that the word *sumptuous* could not do justice. All manner of mountain-and-sea delicacies and fine wine had been prepared.

“Oh, and I've also prepared alcohol and dishes separately for the martial artists.”

Even though she hadn't been asked beforehand, she had prepared separate seating for those keeping watch outside.

Worried they might feel uncomfortable, she had arranged tables in the corners of the pavilion for the righteous, the heterodox, and the demonic factions respectively, so there would be no chance of them coming into conflict.

“Thank you truly for your consideration.”

The Lady of Thousand Flower Tower looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“You saved me in the past, and now you've saved me again.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk looked around at his friends seated at the table and spoke calmly.

“I would love to take undeserved credit before our Tower Lady, but this time, it was accomplished by all three factions—the demonic, the righteous, and the heterodox—joining hands. It is the moment when the trilateral gathering at the pleasure house finally bore fruit.”

It was a historic day when the righteous, the heterodox, and the demonic had joined hands to face a single enemy. And they had done so not through the cult leaders and alliance leaders, but through the power of their successors.

The Lady of Thousand Flower Tower bowed her head politely to the others as well, conveying her gratitude. She offered a particular smile to Jin Ha-ryeong, who had protected her.

“Then please enjoy yourselves.”

Once she excused herself and withdrew, Geom Mugeuk filled everyone's cups.

“Now, a toast in honor of our reunion.”

Geom Mugeuk raised his cup, and everyone drank.

“Today, let's drink and enjoy to our heart's content. Until this drinking session ends, forget all about enemies and schemes! Forget which faction we belong to. Let's just talk about first loves and idle nonsense and have fun!”

Everyone drained their cups in one go.

A sheepish transmission flew from Bisain.

—Don't get the wrong idea.

—What wrong idea?

—The fact that I'm wearing white robes is purely coincidental. I simply changed into whatever was at hand.

Geom Mugeuk stared at Bisain, then made a disappointed expression.

—Why?

—Did you comb your hair yourself?

Bisain flinched, then spoke firmly.

—I didn't!

Soon after, Bisain asked coyly.

—...Does it look strange?

Geom Mugeuk forced back a laugh and drank. Here was a man who had arrived late yet had still taken the time to comb his hair. Despite that fearsome face, despite being the terrifying Young Leader of the Heretical Alliance, he was still a young man in the spring of his youth.

—Now that I look again, I suppose it looks fine.

—Anyway, don't get the wrong idea.

If Han Seol hadn't been to his liking, there was no way he would have come out wearing white robes. Not to mention combing his hair? This wasn't just being pleased with a first impression; was it not love at first sight?

Geom Mugeuk was about to drink again when he flinched in surprise. Bisain was glaring at him fiercely.

—Try teasing me! It'll spark an all-out war that very day.

—I didn't say anything.

—I warned you!

Of course, the warning was ineffective. Just as Geom Mugeuk was beaming, pondering how to tease him so well that word would spread—

“White seems to suit you better.”

Everyone's gaze turned toward the speaker.

—Huh? I didn't see that coming.

Bisain didn't even hear Geom Mugeuk's transmission. The one who had spoken was, surprisingly, Han Seol.

She looked at Bisain and spoke.

“You suit it better than the black robes you wore yesterday.”

Bisain's face flushed slightly, his pupils widening.

At a loss for words, he simply sat there blankly.

—Quick, say thank you!

At Geom Mugeuk's transmission, Bisain clasped his hands respectfully and answered her.

“Thank you for your kind words.”

“I was merely expressing my feelings.”

Bisain sent another transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—You put her up to this, didn't you? To tease me.

His voice was trembling, though he hadn't shaken even when fighting powerful enemies.

—I told you, I'm not that close with Lady Han.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Han Seol with renewed interest. When he had seen her before, she had given the impression of being very closed off in her relationships. She felt somewhat different now.

Perhaps finding the attention focused on her burdensome, she added to her previous words.

“Most people in our palace wear white robes, so we know who white robes suit and who they don't.”

Geom Mugeuk seized upon her words. He couldn't let this golden opportunity slip by.

“Since the subject has come up, could you offer our Young Leader a word of advice on how to wear white robes?”

Han Seol looked at Bisain. At that, Bisain's face grew even redder.

“How could I offer such advice?”

When she politely declined, Geom Mugeuk persuaded her.

“You know, don't you? For people like us, this may be a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity to hear such words. If you don't tell him, Young Leader Bi will wear nothing but dreary black robes his whole life.”

Unable to refuse after he had gone that far, she looked at Bisain for a moment, then spoke calmly.

“You have a strong impression, so you probably wear martial uniforms of intense colors like black or red. But doing so only emphasizes your impression further. Even if not white robes, if you were to wear clothes of bright, warm colors, you would seem much gentler.”

Bisain was speechless for a moment. It was the first time in his life he had heard advice to wear martial robes of warm colors.

Not even once had he thought such robes would suit him—no, he had never even considered wearing them in the first place.

Bisain lowered his head slightly to her and spoke.

“Thank you for your words.”

Han Seol lowered her head slightly as well.

The Twin Swords of Hanbing, seated a short distance from her, exchanged glances. They hadn't known the Young Palace Lady had such a side to her. She had usually been one to simply listen.

—The Palace Lord would be surprised to hear this.

—He wouldn't believe it.

They felt that this was a side of her that the Palace Lord knew nothing about either.

Geom Mugeuk raised his cup again.

“Now, to our Young Leader's splendid martial robes!”

Everyone emptied their cups again.

Setting down his cup, Geom Mugeuk asked Han Seol.

“Lady Han, what is your ideal type?”

At the sudden question, Han Seol wore a surprised expression. Bisain, sitting beside her, glared at Geom Mugeuk.

—Didn't I tell you not to!

Geom Mugeuk continued talking with her, pretending not to notice.

“Why are you so surprised?”

“It's the first time I've been asked that.”

Indeed. Who would have asked the Young Palace Lady of the Ice Palace about her ideal type?

“Why do you ask?”

“Isn't it acceptable for friends to ask that much?”

At the word “friends,” Han Seol's clear eyes shone even brighter.

She seemed to contemplate for a moment.

“I like dependable men. The fewer words, the better.”

“How cruel!”

The quick-witted Jin Ha-ryeong sent a transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—The Young Leader wearing white robes—that was your doing, wasn't it?

He wouldn't have shown up to a gathering like this wearing white robes out of nowhere. There had also been a subtle current flowing at the drinking table.

—It was.

—Trying to set the two of them up?

Even without resorting to a woman's intuition, she knew Geom Mugeuk well enough to guess.

—I think they'd suit each other well.

Hearing that, Jin Ha-ryeong looked at her older brother.

‘What about our brother!’

She was worried he might feel hurt knowing Geom Mugeuk was only looking after Bisain.

Because she knew better than anyone how much she thought of Geom Mugeuk, she had such worries.

—Look after our brother a little too.

—Jin Daeju is your responsibility. You're the one leading the gatherings of the rising stars. You've met hundreds of martial heroines, haven't you?

Jin Ha-ryeong flinched for a moment. Even if not hundreds, she had met many fine martial heroines.

Come to think of it, she had never resolved to find a suitable woman for her older brother.

He would become the Alliance Leader; she had vaguely thought he would meet a better martial heroine later. Honestly, she simply hadn't cared.

Having done so, she couldn't very well ask Geom Mugeuk why he didn't show her brother the same consideration.

She reflected sincerely. Even if they were friends, he was still a man, and a man was showing such care like that.

Jin Ha-ryeong looked at her older brother. What had happened at the Jinbeop? She had felt him grow more pensive after that. Yes, with how busy and difficult things were, when would he have time to court a woman?

‘Yes, I'll find one for you, brother!’

Geom Mugeuk led the atmosphere of the drinking table with various stories while exchanging transmissions with Bisain.

—Don't just sit there drinking; ask Lady Han something too.

—Thank you for the thought, but there's no need.

Honestly, Bisain had liked Han Seol from the first impression. Based on appearance alone, she was his ideal type.

—It's impossible anyway. How could the Ice Palace and the Heretical Alliance ever be connected?

Though the Ice Palace belonged to the mysterious forces beyond the central plains, it was a sect close to the righteous faction.

—Did you and I become friends because we were from compatible factions?

Geom Mugeuk refilled Bisain's empty cup.

—And if it doesn't work, it'll be because of your face, not because of the Ice Palace and the Heretical Alliance. How could it be because of the sects?

Bisain had been about to flare up but let out a hollow laugh instead.

—True enough.

—Conversely, it might work because of your face.

—What do you mean?

—Didn't I tell you before? Your smile is handsome. A woman who thinks like me would fall for that face.

—Nonsense!

He truly thought it nonsense. Even he could see that he looked truly fearsome and fierce.

—If she had no interest at all, she wouldn't have told you earlier that white suits you.

Bisain tilted his cup again.

—If we part ways this time, who knows when we'll meet again.

—I know your game. You want me to confess so you can tease me when I'm rejected?

—Of course, I'm not entirely without such thoughts, but why would I want to watch you ruin this fine drinking session in anger?

Bisain knew he ought not let it pass, yet he let it pass once more.

—Then why are you doing this?

—The martial world is fine, the Heretical Alliance is fine, all of it is fine. But we are also young, aren't we?

—!

These youths did not yet know. How quickly time flowed, and how soon one reached the final destination of life.

—You might remember Lady Han later, but won't you forget what this excitement felt like?

Bisain stared at Geom Mugeuk. He'd never even lived that long, yet he could say such things.

—Have courage. You're the one who even danced before all those people.

Bisain eventually laughed. At least when he recalled that incident, he had no regrets.

—If rejected, I won't even piss toward the Northern Sea! If war breaks out, I'll storm the Northern Sea Ice Palace first! At that time, our cult will come too!

In truth, there was a temptation far stronger than these playful empty words.

That he had never suffered losses or regrets from listening to Geom Mugeuk until now.

Of course, that didn't mean he intended to reveal his feelings to Han Seol. Even if he regretted it later. The memory of Geom Mugeuk persuading him like this was enough. Later, Geom Mugeuk would tease him. Saying, “See, you should have listened to me.” Yes, he might regret it.

But Geom Mugeuk was not a man to give up.

—The night is still young. Now, shall we liven up the mood?

Geom Mugeuk sprang to his feet.

“We weren't the only ones who fought last night.”

Geom Mugeuk's gaze turned toward the Sado Sipsam-rang and the Demon Annihilation Squad standing guard everywhere, and further to the Faceless Guests perched on the far wall.

“We fought together, so we must drink together. At least for today.”

Geom Mugeuk extended his hand, exerting his Vacuum Grasp.

Srrrrk.

He drew the tables and chairs prepared in each corner into one place. The tables laden with food moved quickly, yet not a drop of wine was spilled, and though countless chairs moved, no sound was made.

“Let's all gather and drink together today. Let's share drinks with one another and toast. We don't need perimeter watch today. If they couldn't defeat us sober, how could they defeat us drunk?”

At those words, some martial artists laughed. But they couldn't simply be drinking when the Young Leader and the Demon Annihilation Leader were present.

Then a voice rang out.

“Today, we will protect Thousand Flower Tower.”

Turning around, Geukak Soma was standing at the entrance. Behind him, the Faceless Guests lined up.

Jin Ha-gun and Bisain were surprised and bewildered.

They hadn't been able to drink with Geukak Soma and the Faceless Guests anyway. But even so, they hadn't expected Geukak Soma to say he would protect them.

Geom Mugeuk spoke with a smile.

“Would you do that?”

Geukak Soma smiled from behind his mask.

“Drink to your heart's content today.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Ha-gun and Bisain. With Geom Mugeuk stepping forward, and even Geukak Soma coming forth, there was no need to hesitate further.

The two nodded and called all their subordinates inside.

“Now, drink comfortably today! That is an order!”

The people of Thousand Flower Tower rushed about with alcohol and food.

The Sado Sipsam-rang and the Demon Annihilation Squad sat together. They had carried out operations together before, so their relationship wasn't particularly awkward.

Geom Mugeuk went around pouring wine for everyone.

“Now, drink comfortably today. Put down all your worries and concerns, and celebrate that we survived, at least for today!”

Raucous sounds were heard from inside.

Before long, Geukak Soma was standing atop the highest roof of Thousand Flower Tower, arms crossed.

Below him, the Faceless Guests were scattered across the roofs and walls, standing or crouching as they kept watch on the surroundings.

Moonlight shone through a hole in the broken wall.

Through that hole, the figure of Geom Mugeuk was visible, raising his wine cup high and shouting a toast.

Today, it was not the sword at his waist but the wine cup raised together that shone in the moonlight.

Chapter 555: Care to Get Some Fresh Air with Me?
------------------------------------------------
Han Seol’s astonishment showed no sign of ending.

Fine, she could accept that she had simply arrived at the wrong time, only to find herself in the middle of a bloody fight and even forced to unleash palm force.

Fine, she could accept that the Young Palace Lord of the North Sea Ice Palace might speak of her ideal type in front of the successors of the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions.

But now she was watching the Demon Extermination Unit and the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path—the symbols and elites of the orthodox and unorthodox factions—raising cups together in a toast. Ah, and with the Extreme Evil Young Demon and the Faceless Guest standing guard over them, no less.

This time, Han Seol was the first to send a voice transmission to the Twin Ice Swords.

—Is the Central Plains originally this kind of place? It’s so different from the Central Plains I learned about.

Of course, the Twin Ice Swords knew she was joking, but they too conveyed their own shock. In truth, they were even more astonished.

—As far as I know, in the history of the murim, the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions have never mingled this freely.

—The more it is like this, the more cautious we must be.

They almost said, This may be their scheme, but stopped themselves. To call it a scheme, the scale was far too grand. There was no way all these people had been mobilized merely to trap Han Seol.

Besides, right now, they were far too sincere.

The Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path and the Demon Extermination Unit toasted together and emptied their cups. They even exchanged names with the people seated before them.

“I am Ilrang.”

“I am Gwanghyo.”

If Geom Mugeuk had poured the first cup, this time Bisa-in and Jin Hagun stepped forward together.

Bisa-in poured for the Demon Extermination Unit, and Jin Hagun poured for the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path.

Both those pouring and the subordinates receiving the wine were experiencing something entirely new.

Had this been any other organization of the Murim Alliance or the Evil Path Alliance, such an atmosphere could never have formed. Even if they drank together, it would have been a tense occasion filled with contests of will, each side ready to attack the other at any moment.

But the Demon Extermination Unit and the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path had both overcome a life-and-death crisis with their leaders through Geom Mugeuk’s help. By now, they were also the warriors who best understood that the friendship between these men was genuine.

Next, the wine was poured as it originally should have been. Bisa-in poured for the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path, and Jin Hagun poured for the Demon Extermination Unit. In the eyes directed toward their leaders, deep loyalty and trust were contained.

It felt as though a sturdy bolt had been drawn across the emotions they had built up until now through this battle.

In human relationships, if one never experiences the moment that bolt is secured, even the oldest and finest relationship can collapse in an instant.

Because of a truly trivial word. No, precisely because it is trivial, it collapses all the more emptily.

Was that why? Bisa-in announced one fact to everyone.

“This year marks the thirtieth year since Ilrang here joined the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path.”

When Bisa-in suddenly spoke about him, Ilrang was flustered.

But Bisa-in had something he truly wanted to say here, in front of everyone.

“Leaving aside affiliation and rank, as one martial artist to another, I sincerely respect you.”

When Bisa-in raised his cup, everyone raised theirs as well.

The Thirteen Wolves loudly offered their congratulations, while the Demon Extermination Unit, with proper courtesy, raised their cups and drank in place of words.

Representing them, Jin Hagun said to Ilrang,

“I believe guarding one’s place for thirty years is a great thing. You are truly admirable.”

Because the respect of the Demon Extermination Unit’s captain could be felt, not only Ilrang himself but the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path were delighted.

“Thank you, Captain.”

After Ilrang had expressed his thanks with courtesy, he looked at Bisa-in last. He would have been grateful even if Bisa-in had told him privately, just the two of them, but for him to properly honor him like this in front of others filled him with genuine gratitude. This was an attitude and method Bisa-in had learned from Geom Mugeuk.

“When I was born and just learning to walk, Ilrang, you must already have been walking the path of the Evil Way.”

At Bisa-in’s words, Ilrang laughed, and the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path raised their cups in unison.

“The Thirteen Evil Paths, living together and dying together!”

They shouted loudly and emptied their cups together.

Watching that sight, Jin Hagun felt it.

‘You, too, are looking properly at your subordinates.’

Just as he had looked back at the men of the Demon Extermination Unit when he killed the Heavenly Fiend inside the formation. Had he not looked back then, he would never have been able to speak openly and honestly with his subordinates.

When Jin Hagun lifted his cup high, the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Unit also raised theirs. Since this was a place to congratulate Ilrang, they did not shout to raise their spirits, but merely exchanged gazes and drank.

A consideration that would have been unimaginable in the past. In the past, they would have shouted “Exterminate Demons and Break Evil” even louder than the others.

Jin Hagun looked toward the person who had created this moment.

Geom Mugeuk was speaking with Han Seol at his original seat. He had judged that in this atmosphere of unity, it was right for him to step back.

Han Seol set down her cup together with Geom Mugeuk. Feeling as if she would become too drunk, she had only touched it to her lips before setting it down, but Geom Mugeuk had once again emptied his cup. Geom Mugeuk neither forced her to drink nor stopped her from drinking.

“You can really hold your liquor. It doesn’t seem as if you’re driving out the intoxication with your inner energy either.”

Truly, Geom Mugeuk had been drinking with this person and that person all over the place, yet he did not look drunk.

“Why would I use inner energy to drive away this pleasant tipsiness?”

“You have quite the tolerance.”

“You know, don’t you? My elder brother is the Drunken Demon Venerable.”

Since he had once come to the North Sea with the Drunken Demon, she knew about the relationship between the two.

Pouring wine for Geom Mugeuk, Han Seol asked,

“Is Sister doing well?”

She had thought she would be able to meet Ian if she came here.

At that, Geom Mugeuk gave an unexpected answer.

“I don’t know either.”

“Pardon?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned far out beyond them.

“Ian has gone out into the world alone.”

Knowing that he regarded Ian as his heart, Han Seol was surprised that he had let her go out on her own.

“Aren’t you worried?”

“How could I not be? She’s become a ghost and appears in my dreams every night.”

“Then why did you let her go alone?”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said,

“She has to know how to walk alone too.”

They were ordinary words, but within those words of Geom Mugeuk’s lay the life he had lived before his regression.

Because he had personally experienced that the harsher the path, the stronger one returned.

The moment Han Seol heard those words, her heart began to race. She, too, felt the desire to walk the world alone.

“That’s a shame. I thought I would be able to see her this time.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“See her before you go.”

“Pardon?”

“It is about time for her to return to the main cult. She may already be back. Come to the main cult with me. I will formally invite you.”

Before she could answer, the voice transmissions of the Twin Ice Swords flew into her ears.

—The Divine Cult is absolutely out of the question!

—Our palace has never visited the Divine Cult!

Rather, those voice transmissions strengthened her decision.

—Then let my journey to the Central Plains be the first.

Han Seol wanted to visit the headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Since they had come to the North Sea Ice Palace, was it not only right that she go as well?

And above all, she wanted to see Ian. She wanted to ask her what she had seen after going out into the world alone.

“I once asked my mother what kind of place the Central Plains was to her. At the time, she told me this: it was a place you wanted to return from once you went there, and once you returned, a place you wanted to go back to again. I wonder what kind of place the Central Plains will become for me.”

Because it was an official invitation, she cupped her fist respectfully and said,

“I accept your invitation. Thank you for inviting me.”

Geom Mugeuk also cupped his fist respectfully and said,

“I hope our cult can become a good memory in the Young Palace Lord’s Central Plains.”

And so Han Seol’s journey to the Divine Cult was decided.

* * *

The night deepened, and so did the drinking.

Everyone was drunk.

Of course, that did not mean the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path or the Demon Extermination Unit made mistakes with one another. They were elite warriors, and men who served successors at that, so even while drinking, they were cautious and cautious again.

Geom Mugeuk drank with Jin Hagun and Bisa-in, while Jin Haryeong and Han Seol drank with the mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion. Whatever conversation they were enjoying so much, those two young women were surely hearing words today that would be of great help in their lives.

Geom Mugeuk called out to the musicians playing from the second-floor railing.

“Please give us something more exciting!”

The gentle music quickened, and the mood naturally grew lively.

When Geom Mugeuk began swaying his body lightly, Bisa-in shouted first.

“Don’t even dream of it!”

But Geom Mugeuk, like someone starting his engine in earnest, slowly began to make his movements bigger.
