When Geom Mugeuk looked toward Han Seol, Bisa-in beat him to the punch again.

“The moment the word dancing comes up, the Evil-Demonic War breaks out!”

Geom Mugeuk looked regretful.

“If Lady Han saw your dancing skills, she would fall for you even more.”

His words alone sounded regretful, but his body looked as if it would leap up and start shaking at any moment. Would Geom Mugeuk dance alone? If he said the true dancer was over there, there was no doubt Bisa-in would eventually be dragged out.

Driven into a corner, Bisa-in looked at Jin Hagun. His gaze said that if he did not stop this, it would not be an Evil-Demonic War but an Evil-Orthodox War that broke out, yet Jin Hagun, his face flushed with drink, said something entirely unexpected.

“If you fellows dance, I will at least perform a sword dance.”

He meant that even if he could not do an ordinary dance, he could manage a sword dance.

Bisa-in stared at Jin Hagun with a shocked expression. It was within expectations for Geom Mugeuk to tease him, but Jin Hagun said he would perform a sword dance?

“You’ve all gone mad! You’re far too drunk!”

Now that even Jin Hagun had joined in, wings had been added to Geom Mugeuk’s swaying. He would somehow create an atmosphere where dancing happened.

Bisa-in shot to his feet.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him in surprise and said,

“You’re going to dance?”

Of course, as if that would happen.

Bisa-in strode over to Han Seol and said,

“Would you step outside for some air with me?”

Since this was not something that could be solved by simply running away, he had chosen a frontal breakthrough.

At those words, Han Seol was flustered, and Jin Haryeong was even more surprised.

The Young Alliance Leader was asking a woman to take a walk?

Jin Haryeong looked at Geom Mugeuk and sent a voice transmission.

—Just what did you do?

—I’m wronged! I didn’t say anything.

—Because of you, the Young Alliance Leader has become strange.

She should have heard her elder brother say he would perform a sword dance.

Was Han Seol being considerate of the gazes watching them? Or did she truly feel like taking a walk?

After quietly looking at Bisa-in, Han Seol answered calmly.

“I was feeling stifled from the intoxication anyway.”

Han Seol rose from her seat and left the building with Bisa-in.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission flew to Bisa-in.

—You’re a man. A real man!

—And whose fault is all this!

Riding on a sigh, the voice transmission reached him, and the playfulness faded from Geom Mugeuk’s reply.

—Even after many years pass, you won’t forget the thrill you felt when asking someone to take a walk.

Bisa-in knew. Geom Mugeuk truly was cheering him on and pushing him forward out of sincere concern for him.

—If war breaks out, I’m invading the Divine Cult first! To hell with the North Sea!

And so the two of them went outside.

Watching them, Geom Mugeuk raised his cup high.

“Now, let’s drink a toast to victory!”

Was it truly a toast to victory? Jin Hagun looked at Bisa-in with concern. The only thing that suited them was their clothing; he wondered if Bisa-in would even manage to say a single proper word.

After the two of them drank a few more cups like that, Jin Hagun revealed the thought he had been carrying in his heart.

“When I return this time, I intend to speak to my grandfather. I will ask him to decide on me as his successor.”

Without a word, Geom Mugeuk filled Jin Hagun’s cup. Jin Hagun’s true feelings came out calmly.

“Until now, I have lived while hiding my desires too much. I thought that was chivalry and the righteous path. I thought if I lived that way, everything would naturally come to pass. But… I began to think that this too may have been an escape from reality. An escape I postponed again and again because I did not want my grandfather to notice my greed to become the next Alliance Leader. Compared to saying those words, it was easier to become the captain of the Demon Extermination Unit and fight.”

Listening to him, Geom Mugeuk quietly drank.

Perhaps the Murim Alliance Leader felt the same.

The burden of making his grandson his successor. The burden borne by the Murim Alliance Leader, who had pursued only honor his entire life.

Because he could not endure the dishonor of making his grandson the successor when there might be someone more capable, perhaps he too had been escaping reality.

Thinking vaguely that if his grandson served for a long time as captain of the Demon Extermination Unit, a moment would come when everyone acknowledged him.

Geom Mugeuk thought Jin Hagun’s decision was a good thing.

The most dangerous relationship is one in which neither side is honest about their desires. When such a relationship goes awry, the distance between them grows beyond control.

“I also think it is time for your lava to erupt.”

Jin Hagun gained strength from Geom Mugeuk’s support.

“I intend to stake my life on it.”

“No! Are you saying you still haven’t? Even neighborhood hoodlums stake their lives to become boss, and this is about becoming the Murim Alliance Leader. You should have staked it already! Go to the Alliance Leader and shout, Please give me the successor’s seat now! If he scolds you, go flatter him the next day! Just show him your transparent heart in full!”

Jin Hagun laughed. Yes, when he spoke with Geom Mugeuk, strength came to him like this.

Just then, in the distance, they saw Bisa-in and Han Seol walking together.

“What do you think they’re talking about?”

“A secret discussion about the Evil Path Alliance and the North Sea Ice Palace joining hands to strike the Murim Alliance?”

“Didn’t you hear earlier? If they strike anyone, it’ll be you.”

“No chance of that. I’m the one who tied the red thread between them.”

Jin Hagun turned to Geom Mugeuk and said worriedly,

“What will you do if that friend ends up hurt?”

“Then he gets hurt.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Hagun and said calmly,

“When we look back on our lives later, if all we remember is fighting enemies, wouldn’t we feel too sorry toward our own lives?”

Chapter 556: Like the Sun, Like the Wind.
-----------------------------------------
The night air was clear and cold.

Bi Sa-in and Han Seol walked together through the inner garden of Cheonhwaru.

To her, even this stroll was an extension of astonishment. When she had left the Ice Palace, could she ever have imagined she would end up walking beneath the moonlight, alone with the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance?

“Watch your step there.”

The battle of the previous night had left parts of Cheonhwaru broken and damaged. Bi Sa-in felt inwardly sorry to her for dragging someone who had been drinking perfectly well out for a walk in a place like this. He had come out far too recklessly, afraid someone might tell him to dance.

“I apologize if I startled you.”

At Bi Sa-in’s apology, Han Seol shook her head.

“No. I really did want some fresh air. I drank more than I can handle.”

Her face, too, was flushed red with drink.

The two of them stopped walking for a moment. The Faceless Guests on the nearby roof tactfully withdrew. The Cold-Ice Twin Swords also stood at a distance, not disturbing them.

“The moon is very bright tonight.”

At her words, Bi Sa-in also looked up at the night sky. As if that dreadful darkness had never covered the world, the moon and stars were in harmony tonight, beautifully embroidering the sky.

An awkward silence flowed between them for a while.

Bi Sa-in wanted to make a good impression on her. He wanted to be impressive, without misspeaking. Perhaps because that desire weighed on him, the words would not come easily.

‘I have to treat her comfortably! Naturally.’

The more he thought that, the harder his heart pounded. It was his first time experiencing this.

The first to speak was Han Seol.

“When I received the young cult leader’s letter asking me to meet here, I was honestly surprised. I never thought he would truly invite me to a gathering with his friends.”

If he had been close to her, Bi Sa-in would have said this: He is a friend whose pleasure in life is surprising people.

“He is the kind of friend who always keeps the words he speaks.”

“Last time, our palace received great help from the young cult leader. If not for his invitation, Mother would never have allowed me to come to the Central Plains.”

She said it was because he had helped the Ice Palace, but she could feel it. What had moved her mother’s heart even more than that was the fact that he had melted the frozen relationship between her and her mother.

While Han Seol was briefly thinking of the Palace Lord of the Ice Palace, Bi Sa-in was stealing glances at her.

In his eyes, she was far too beautiful. Objectively speaking, Ian was much more beautiful, and Jin Haryeong was also more beautiful.

But when he saw them, he merely thought they were beautiful, whereas when he looked at Han Seol, his heart fluttered. For the first time, he realized that it truly was possible to fall in love at first sight.

But that did not mean he could confess his feelings to her.

No, he could confess. But what came after that?

He was someone who had to lead the Sado Alliance, and she would have to return to the North Sea Ice Palace. There was no way the Palace Lord of the Ice Palace would permit such a relationship. The leader of the Sado Alliance, too, would surely find it displeasing.

‘Yes, this is not the time to be distracted by a woman.’

His head thought so, but his gaze kept turning toward her.

“Shall we go over there?”

She looked toward the path leading to the rear garden.

“Let us.”

The two of them moved toward the rear garden.

On the way, Bi Sa-in failed to bring up any interesting conversation. In the end, he admitted it honestly.

“I apologize. I’m not good with words.”

“It’s all right. I’m the same.”

Bi Sa-in was curious. She had said that her ideal type was a dependable, taciturn man. He could not tell whether she had said it merely to tease Geom Mugeuk, or whether she had meant it sincerely.

The two of them walked in silence like that until they stopped before a place where part of the wall had collapsed.

“What villain could have knocked down such a beautiful wall?”

At her regretful words, Bi Sa-in said with a flinch on his face.

“Me.”

“Pardon?”

“I knocked it down while fighting here.”

She had said it to break the awkward silence, but of all places, it happened to be the part Bi Sa-in had destroyed.

Han Seol looked again at the collapsed wall and said,

“It might be nice to make a gate here.”

At those words, Bi Sa-in laughed. Han Seol laughed along with him.

At the sight of her smiling, Bi Sa-in turned his head without realizing it. It felt like looking at bright white snow, so dazzling he could not face it directly.

In any case, thanks to that, the awkwardness faded.

“Ah, I was invited to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Bi Sa-in calmly nodded. Since they had invited the young palace lord of the Ice Palace, they would not simply send her away.

“It will be interesting if you go.”

Truthfully, he wanted to go with her himself. If he followed his heart, the words “Let me go with you!” had risen all the way to his throat. If he blurted them out, then perhaps, as he did with Geom Mugeuk, several jokes might follow.

In the meantime, the two of them had completed a circuit around the Cheonhwaru building.

‘Ah, already!’

Bi Sa-in felt terribly disappointed. He had not said even one thing properly. But that did not mean he could ask her to take another lap of that awkward stroll.

“It was enjoyable.”

“I enjoyed it as well.”

Han Seol walked back toward the building where the drinking party was continuing.

“Miss Han.”

“Yes?”

Bi Sa-in hesitated for a moment, then mustered his courage and said,

“From time to time, try wearing martial robes in a warmer color.”

Feeling the tremor in Bi Sa-in’s voice, a faint smile brushed Han Seol’s lips.

“I think they would suit you well.”

She was about to turn away, then added,

“Ah, and I’m going to the Divine Cult to meet my elder sister.”

She entered the building.

Bi Sa-in stood there blankly for a moment, then looked up at the roof and said,

“Stop spying and come out.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun, who had been hiding on the roof, revealed themselves.

The two of them sat side by side on the edge of the roof.

“I don’t think we’ll have to worry about the Sado Alliance in the future. The man who’s going to become its leader is that slow on the uptake. Whew, I nearly died of frustration.”

Ignoring Geom Mugeuk, Bi Sa-in spoke to Jin Hagun.

“That friend has always been like that, but why are you acting this way too?”

Jin Hagun smiled awkwardly. He had been dragged out by Geom Mugeuk, who said they should go eavesdrop on what was being said, but could he really say he had been dragged by force? Honestly, he had come because he thought it would be amusing.

Geom Mugeuk answered in his stead.

“Our Captain Jin should enjoy a little deviation sometimes too. How can a man live as a righteous hero every single day?”

“This doesn’t look like deviation. It looks like a model student being swept away by a bad friend and falling into corruption.”

“You speak so well now? You should’ve done that earlier!”

Geom Mugeuk showed the wine bottle he had brought.

“Come on, get up here! I’ll teach you about women! What is a woman?”

“No need. I have no desire to hear such a lecture from someone who doesn’t even know where his own heart is.”

Bi Sa-in lightly launched himself up and sat with them at the end of the eaves.

Perhaps because he had been burning inside, he gulped down wine straight from the bottle before finally letting out a long sigh.

Even now his heart was pounding. No, he was more nervous now than when he had been with her. What did she think of that stroll? Had he ruined everything by calling her out for no reason?

Geom Mugeuk said to Bi Sa-in,

“I have taken a liking to you. Were those words so difficult?”

“And after that?”

If it was this Geom Mugeuk, might he have some kind of plan? Could he give an answer?

This time, Geom Mugeuk drank straight from the bottle.

“That’s something to worry about when the time comes. There’s no need to wear a straw raincoat starting today just because you’re worried it might rain tomorrow, is there?”

Jin Hagun smiled faintly, and Bi Sa-in took the wine bottle from Geom Mugeuk’s hand and drank.

“It’s easy for you to say when it isn’t your affair.”

“That is why you should listen to those words. To the words spoken so easily.”

Bi Sa-in’s hand, which had been drinking, stopped. When he looked at him with an expression asking what he meant, Geom Mugeuk answered,

“Because your heart must be far too complicated right now.”

He was right. All sorts of worries had been rising within his heart.

The wine bottle that had stopped tilted again. He found himself thinking that he, too, did not really know what was right.

“Don’t worry. The red thread between you two has not yet been cut. I tied it together with Heavenly Silkworm silk.”

Just then, the first rooster crowed in the distance, and the pale light of dawn began to brighten the world.

“This night is passing too.”

The three of them felt regretful. They knew that once day broke, each of them would have to go his own way again.

If they followed their hearts, they would have liked to play for days on end, but they could not. They were all important people, busy people. And above all, Cheonhwaru had to be put back in order.

“Ah! I want to play more! I want to keep playing!”

Geom Mugeuk’s shout cut through the dawn air, speaking for all their hearts.

* * *

The next morning, the lord of Cheonhwaru treated them to their final meal.

Everyone had drunk too much the night before, and the hangover soup she had carefully prepared was wonderfully delicious.

After the meal, they shared a reluctant farewell.

It was not only the three friends who felt that way; their subordinate warriors did as well. They knew that as they lived on, opportunities like this would not come often. Rather, they thought the moment when they would have to point blades at one another might come first.

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun and Bi Sa-in,

“In the future, if you fall into danger, call me without delay. You understand, don’t you? Not calling because it’s dangerous isn’t friendship.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Bi Sa-in threw out a farewell joke.

“If another dreadful bastard like the one we fought appears, I’ll be sure to call you.”

Geom Mugeuk flinched and discreetly stepped back.

“In that case, it’s also fine to call Captain Jin first.”

Bi Sa-in said farewell to his two friends like that, and also conveyed his sincere gratitude to Geugak Soma. Thanks to them having protected him yesterday, he had been able to share old feelings with his subordinates.

And today, the farewell he regretted most was with Han Seol. Of course, he did not show it outwardly at all.

“I hope you enjoy your tour of the Central Plains and return safely.”

“Thank you. Young Alliance Leader, please take care of yourself as well.”

Bi Sa-in, who had been about to turn away, abruptly said,

“You may stop by our alliance on your way back. There are quite a few places nearby with fine scenery. If you send word when you are in the area, I will come out to meet you.”

After speaking rapidly, he turned away. The words he had been wavering over whether to say or not had slipped out before he knew it.

‘Ah, I’ve caused trouble.’

With his back turned, he breathed heavily. And in front of so many people watching, at that.

To avoid letting anyone notice that he was flustered, he strode away without even hearing her answer. His pace was so fast that the Thirteen Wolves of Sado were startled and hurried after him.

Then Han Seol’s voice came from behind him.

“I will.”

In that instant, Bi Sa-in stopped walking and looked back at her. Han Seol smiled as she said,

“I’ll stop by on my way back.”

Bi Sa-in forcibly held back the way his face was about to brighten with joy. Han Seol read Bi Sa-in’s feelings from that expression.

“Then I will see you then.”

Bi Sa-in, too, departed with the Thirteen Wolves of Sado like that.

Geom Mugeuk desperately held back the urge to send him a voice transmission and tease him. Let him fully savor this happiness.

Instead, he sent a voice transmission to Jin Hagun.

—What if he’s actually a playboy, and he’s been deceiving us?

At that transmission, Jin Hagun laughed. From what he could see, all three of them were far from being playboys.

Geom Mugeuk also said farewell to Jin Hagun.

“I’ll be waiting for good news.”

Jin Hagun knew what those words meant. They must mean news that he had formally become the successor. Yes, he, too, wanted to deliver that news to Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sa-in.

“Even if it ends up being bad news, I won’t regret it.”

The person silently watching the two of them part was Jin Haryeong.

She, too, felt regretful. If she followed her heart, she would have liked to go to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult together with Han Seol, but she could not. It also wounded her pride to feel as though she were pointlessly hovering around Geom Mugeuk.

Just then, the lord of Cheonhwaru approached her as she stood apart from the others.

“Miss Jin.”

“Ah, Pavilion Lord. Thank you for your kind words yesterday.”

“I enjoyed myself for the first time in a long while as well.”

As they had shared many stories yesterday, the lord of Cheonhwaru had sensed to some extent what Jin Haryeong felt for Geom Mugeuk. No, Jin Haryeong had deliberately revealed her feelings under the pretext of drunkenness. She had wanted to confess her heart to someone. Not to someone around her, but to someone she might see once and never see again.

After exchanging greetings like that, the two of them stood side by side and watched Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun talking.

Then, suddenly, something surged up in Jin Haryeong’s heart. At this moment of parting, she abruptly brought out words she had not spoken even during the drinking party.

“Until now, I was always the main character. The granddaughter of the Murim Alliance Leader, the greatest beauty of Hubei—wherever I went, I always stood at the center. When I spoke, people listened, and everyone looked only at me. Even now, it is still that way. But……”

“Yesterday, throughout the entire drinking party, I felt as if I’d become a supporting character hovering around the protagonist.”

It was something she could never have said to anyone else. And yet, strangely, the words in her heart came easily before the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Mistress. She had felt it since yesterday—there was an odd sense of trust this woman gave her.

After a brief pause, the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Mistress asked,

“How do you feel when you look at the Young Cult Leader? Does he seem like the protagonist?”

“That man is always the protagonist.”

“Do you really think so?”

“Pardon?”

Geom Mugeuk, having finished exchanging greetings with Jin Hagun, was speaking one by one with the warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad. He was joking with them, saying that their squad leader had become so impressive lately that it was almost burdensome.

In that instant, Jin Haryeong understood what the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Mistress had meant by those words.

‘Ah, come to think of it, that man is always trying to make someone else the protagonist.’

Thinking back, it had been the same when he first met her. He had been Seo Daeryong’s attendant.

“He may look like the sun, but I think the Young Cult Leader is someone like the wind.”

Suddenly, an image rose in Jin Haryeong’s mind: Geom Mugeuk drinking alone. She did not know why such a vision had come to her.

The Cheonhwaru Pavilion Mistress looked at Jin Haryeong.

“If you choose to journey with the wind, Miss Jin, it will be very difficult for you from now on. But it won’t be because of your pretty heart, nor because of the difference in your statuses. It will be because the wind cannot be grasped in your hand.”

With trembling eyes, Jin Haryeong silently gazed at the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Mistress. Those words had become a signpost for her tangled heart, which had not known where to turn.

“May I come visit again next time? Alone, then.”

“Anytime.”

Jin Haryeong bowed her head politely, conveying her gratitude.

Then she called out loudly to Jin Hagun.

“Brother, this farewell is taking too long! Let’s go already.”

And to Geom Mugeuk as well, she spoke with spirited confidence.

“Take good care of Miss Han!”

She also bade Han Seol farewell in a loud voice.

“Miss Han, not all men of the Central Plains are that talkative. Please don’t misunderstand!”

And so Jin Hagun, Jin Haryeong, and the Demon Extermination Squad left Cheonhwaru.

Then came the final farewell of the day.

The Great Evil Young Demon and the Faceless Guests said they would remain at Cheonhwaru.

“Please return first. I will stay at Cheonhwaru a while longer before going back.”

The Great Evil Young Demon would remain to rebuild the damaged Cheonhwaru buildings and establish his own defenses so that such a thing would not happen again.

The Great Evil Young Demon returned the freshly washed supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Thread. As Geom Mugeuk wound it around the hilt of the Black Demon Sword, he said,

“We survived this fight too.”

Then the Great Evil Young Demon spoke in a low voice, his sincerity flowing through it.

“It was an honor to fight alongside you.”

Geom Mugeuk also conveyed his sincerity to him.

“I was able to live thanks to you, Soma-nim.”

Geom Mugeuk greeted him respectfully, with the courtesy due to a Demon Venerable.

“Then I will see you at the main cult.”

At that, the Faceless Guests lined up behind the Great Evil Young Demon also bowed in unison with the courtesy due to the Young Cult Leader.

The eyes behind their masks were smiling. Eyes filled with goodwill. Among the gains from this journey, the Faceless Guests could not be left out.

Having said farewell to everyone, Geom Mugeuk turned back to Han Seol.

“Now then, shall we go taste the spiciest, most stimulating, and most dangerously perilous flavor in the Central Plains?”

Chapter 557: Demonkind Does Not Part.
-------------------------------------
Seo Daeryong was waiting in the safe house together with the Dan sisters.

Thanks to receiving treatment here continuously, their injuries had almost completely healed. Now, they only had to wait for news from Geom Mugeuk.

During that time, he spent a great deal of time with Dan A.

They talked about all sorts of things, from their pasts to martial arts.

And through those conversations, Seo Daeryong became certain.

This was the woman!

He had a feeling that if he let Dan A slip away, he would regret it for the rest of his life.

But he could not confess his feelings to her. They were recovering from their injuries in a safe house belonging to the Demonic Cult, so he could not make such a confession to her. It would be no different from pressuring her with her life as collateral.

The problem was that things would be no different even after they had fully recovered. She was a Pavilion Master of the Demonic Cult, after all. How burdensome would it be for her?

That was why this relationship was so difficult. If only he had not known her identity and could simply recite poetry to her as before. Those days had truly been wonderful.

That afternoon, a messenger from the safe house delivered a letter.

After checking its contents, Seo Daeryong went to find the Dan sisters, his face shining.

“News has arrived from the Young Cult Master. He has dealt with all of them.”

The Dan sisters rejoiced. The youngest, Dan Yeon, even let out a cheer.

“You are all safe now.”

But Dan A’s eyes soon darkened.

This news meant that she would soon have to part with Seo Daeryong.

Silence flowed through the room.

To be honest, Dan A had even considered following Seo Daeryong and living a life with him.

A Yellow Springs Pavilion Master who read poetry collections.

A man who honestly told her about his gloomy, painful past, and who strove to escape from it.

The more she spoke with him, the more she realized what a pure person he was. Somehow, she felt that she could be happy building a life together with this man. No—she wanted to.

But she could not part with her sisters. The three of them had survived by joining forces until now.

If she left them behind, would her sisters be able to survive in this martial world?

She would never be able to live happily, worrying about them every day.

Yet she could not force her sisters to become demonic practitioners as well. That would mean too great a change in their fates.

“We should be leaving now. Thank you so much for saving us.”

Seo Daeryong was flustered. Secretly, he had hoped that perhaps she might go with him to the Demonic Cult. But it seemed she had already decided to leave. He had not expected their farewell to come so suddenly.

Dan A gazed at Seo Daeryong with tender eyes.

‘I’m sorry. I have to protect my sisters.’

After all the conversations they had shared over the past few days, he knew well how she had lived. He understood how deeply she cared for her sisters.

Seo Daeryong looked away, afraid that the tears welling up from his disappointment might spill over. But in the end, tears streamed down his face.

Seeing Seo Daeryong cry, Dan A felt tears rising in her own eyes. But she clenched her teeth and held them back. If her sisters saw her grieving, their hearts would ache.

“Thank you so much, Pavilion Master Seo.”

She forced herself to bid him farewell in a bright voice.

Seo Daeryong knew that if he let her go now, it would be the end forever. If they were truly destined to meet again, then things would have worked out even now. He had to hold on to her. Even if he had to force her, even if he had to beg.

That was when he heard the youngest, Dan Yeon, call out.

“We’ll pack our things, Sister.”

The two younger sisters immediately began packing. There was hardly anything they could call luggage, but they began placing this and that into their storage pouches.

At the sight, the last of Seo Daeryong’s courage to stop Dan A collapsed.

A wave of sorrow swept over him.

He had grown close to her two sisters as well. No—he had merely mistaken himself for being close to them.

He had been so happy when the youngest lady had called him “brother-in-law.” Of course, he knew she had been joking, but he had still been delighted. He must have become intoxicated by that joke and misunderstood the situation.

‘Yes, who am I to think of taking a woman?’

The moment his heart weakened, the evil spirit whispered into his ear.

―Do you really think a wonderful woman like that could ever like you? She only pretended to like you because you were a Pavilion Master of the Cult. Are you an idiot? Did you think reciting one poem would be enough? Look at Donggyeong! Look at yourself!

Dan A’s heart ached terribly at the sight of Seo Daeryong suffering.

Unable to bear looking at him, she left the room first.

“I hope you accomplish great things! I will cheer you on from afar.”

Dan A left the room.

‘Yes, who am I to think of taking a man?’

Just as she had lived until now, she would survive in this harsh martial world by helping her sisters and receiving their help in return. She had lived well enough without a man until now.

She had just passed through the door of the safe house when—

“Where are you going?”

The one calling out to her from behind was the youngest, Dan Yeon.

When Dan A turned around, Dan Bi and Dan Yeon were standing beside the carriage.

“You have to ride this.”

“What?”

Dan Yeon spoke to Seo Daeryong, who had followed them outside and was standing there blankly.

“You’re going to the Cult in this carriage, right?”

Seo Daeryong nodded with a bewildered expression, and Dan Yeon dropped a bombshell.

“My second sister and I want to devote ourselves to the Demonic Cult.”

Both Seo Daeryong and Dan A were startled.

“Will you accept us?”

Flustered, Seo Daeryong looked back and forth between Dan A and her two sisters.

Dan A hurriedly spoke to him.

“Don’t mind her. My sister is only joking.”

“I’m not joking, Sister.”

“Stop it! Even if you are joking, that is an improper thing to say to Pavilion Master Seo.”

Dan Yeon’s expression was serious.

“We’re not joking.”

Then Dan Bi, the second sister, spoke for the first time.

“It isn’t only because of Sister.”

Everyone’s eyes turned toward Dan Bi.

“Living as wandering fighters has become too difficult. I’m tired.”

Dan A could tell that her sister was speaking sincerely. Dan Bi was not the sort of person to say something she did not mean about matters like this.

“I want to settle down and live somewhere now.”

Dan A understood Dan Bi’s feelings. How much had the three of them gone through, and how much suffering had they endured, to survive as wandering fighters? As for herself, she had been so focused on taking care of her sisters that she had not even realized her own hardships were hardships. But her sisters must have felt differently.

“Our goal was to save money and settle down, wasn’t it? The time has come.”

A brief silence followed.

Dan A looked at the youngest. In response to her gaze, which asked if Dan Yeon felt the same way, Dan Yeon answered with a question.

“Can you accept us?”

Seo Daeryong was so happy that he wanted to shout. He felt utterly pathetic for having harbored even a moment of resentment toward these sisters.

“Youngest Lady, I don’t know if you can tell from my appearance, which doesn’t make me look very authoritative, but the Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion holds a fairly high position.”

He had called it “fairly” out of humility. The Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion stood at the very center of power.

Dan Yeon let out a joyful cheer.

“Today is the first day of my life as a demonic practitioner!”

Even Dan Bi, who rarely showed her emotions, smiled.

Dan A knew that although her sisters truly were tired and weary, their decision to become demonic practitioners was because of her.

“Are you really sure? You will have to live as demonic practitioners from now on.”

At her calm question, Dan Bi answered,

“We survived as wandering fighters from the very bottom. Why couldn’t we live as demonic practitioners?”

Dan Yeon revealed her usual optimistic nature.

“If it gets too hard, the three of us can flee in the middle of the night! We’re incredibly good at running away, you know!”

She had meant it as a joke, but tears gathered in Dan A’s eyes. How could she not know what her sisters were feeling?

Dan Bi delivered the decisive words.

“Such a great sacrifice would burden us. What will you do when the time comes for us to leave someday? Don’t do this for our sake. We can all live well on our own.”

Dan A fell into thought for a moment before raising her head. Her expression made it clear that she had reached a decision.

Seo Daeryong looked at her with pleading eyes. He only hoped she would not say, “I’m sorry, but I can’t.”

After taking a deep breath, Dan A spoke with determination.

“Pavilion Master Seo, the Three Dan Sisters will devote themselves to the Demonic Cult!”

In that instant, Seo Daeryong threw both arms into the air. He was so happy that no shout would come out.

This was not simply the moment when three new disciples joined the Demonic Cult.

It was the moment when a woman had appeared who was willing to accept him as a man—while even devoting herself to the Demonic Cult for his sake.

He had never once been chosen by a woman in his life. Yet such a wonderful woman had accepted his feelings.

“I’ve already told you about myself. Knowing that I’m like this, are you still all right with me?”

Dan A gave him her characteristic, meaningful smile and replied,

“You still don’t know everything about me. I probably have plenty of bad sides I’ve kept hidden. Are you sure you’re all right with someone like me?”

Seo Daeryong nodded.

“I’m all right with it.”

The youngest, who had been listening, spoke up.

“You can’t take it back, Brother-in-law!”

At the words “brother-in-law,” Seo Daeryong’s face flushed red.

“I absolutely won’t take it back, Sister-in-law.”

He had summoned all his courage to use the word for the first time. The tips of Seo Daeryong’s ears turned red.

Then Dan Bi said impassively,

“But what if you two become this close and then break up?”

The youngest imitated a deep voice as she answered,

“We demonic practitioners do not break up. We only kill.”

Seo Daeryong waved both hands frantically.

“That’s a misunderstanding! The Cult isn’t like that!”

Dan Bi and Dan Yeon climbed into the carriage first.

Seo Daeryong conveyed his true feelings to Dan A.

“I will protect you for the rest of my life.”

Dan A smiled.

“Now we have to protect each other.”

Seo Daeryong was so happy he felt as though he could fly. It was truly like a dream. He wanted to see Geom Mugeuk at once and boast about it. He wanted to brag all night in front of Ian and Jang Ho.

As Dan A passed by Seo Daeryong to board the carriage, she spoke softly.

“And I will kill you.”

“What?”

He turned around in surprise, only to see the youngest smiling.

“I clearly said there would be no taking it back!”

The three women in the carriage smiled as they looked at Seo Daeryong.

Seo Daeryong felt that a new life had begun. When he had left the Cult to meet his master for the first time, he had been alone. Now he was returning as four.

As he climbed onto the coachman’s seat, Seo Daeryong said,

“I won’t take it back, if only because of my sister-in-law.”

* * *

Another carriage carrying a different fate was also racing swiftly toward the Demonic Cult.

Throughout the journey, the Hanbing Twin Swords, who were responsible for Han Seol’s protection, remained tense.

The Central Plains martial world, where countless rivalries and conspiracies ran rampant, was dangerous enough. But the Demonic Cult?

They had tried to dissuade her, saying that anywhere but the Demonic Cult would be acceptable, but Han Seol had refused to listen.

The reason the Hanbing Twin Swords had not continued trying to stop her was Geom Mugeuk. They knew that Geom Mugeuk was a great benefactor of the Northern Sea Ice Palace and had earned the Palace Lord’s trust.

―Will she be all right?

―We must protect her well.

The Hanbing Twin Swords were husband and wife.

The husband was Sahan, and the wife was Sobing. The name Hanbing Twin Swords was an alias formed from their names.

They were masters who had made their name in the Northern Sea martial world as a married couple for many years.

The reason the Palace Lord of the Northern Sea Ice Palace trusted them was not only because of their skill, but also because they had lived as a loving couple for a very long time. The Palace Lord believed that a husband and wife who knew how to respect each other could be trusted.

Although they were returning to the Demonic Cult as quickly as possible, whenever they came upon a place with beautiful scenery, Geom Mugeuk always had the carriage stop so he could show it to Han Seol.

“It’s amazing.”

Han Seol gazed up at the sheer cliff as it curved into the sky, filled with admiration. It was truly an overwhelming spectacle.

But he did not show her only magnificent scenery.

He also took her to historic sites where famous masters had fought, and to places where she could see things unavailable in the Northern Sea.

“What you hear and what you see with your own eyes are very different. I hope this journey of yours through the Central Plains becomes one where you see many things, Miss Han.”

Han Seol clearly felt that Geom Mugeuk’s thoughtfulness was different.

If she had invited someone to the Northern Sea Ice Palace, she would have hurried to show them the palace. The Ice Palace was so magnificent, so splendid, and so powerful.

But Geom Mugeuk placed more meaning on her first journey through the Central Plains than on the fact that he had invited her to the Cult.

He sincerely wanted her to see many things before she returned home.

“Now, repeat after me! ‘I’m going to see many things!’”

When Han Seol hesitated, Geom Mugeuk spoke.

“Thinking something and saying it aloud are different. Come, repeat after me.”

After he continued urging her, Han Seol finally gave in and said,

“I’m going to see many things!”

Whenever they stopped at an inn or restaurant with a cook renowned for his skills, they made sure to visit that place as well.

On such occasions, Geom Mugeuk usually invited the Hanbing Twin Swords.

“You two will enjoy this dish.”

“You needn’t trouble yourself over us.”

“Please eat food suited to the tastes of the Young Cult Master and the Young Palace Lord.”

To their refusal, Geom Mugeuk replied,

“We’re young. We can even chew on stones.”

Han Seol nodded when Geom Mugeuk looked to her for agreement. It was the moment when the picky eater became a youth capable of chewing stones.

The dishes Geom Mugeuk treated them to suited the Hanbing Twin Swords’ tastes perfectly.

They tried this, then that. Everything was something they had never tasted in the Northern Sea.

Thanks to him, they were discovering the joys of Central Plains cuisine.

―They are still demonic practitioners, so let’s remain cautious.

―Agreed. Who knows when they might turn on us after putting on such kind faces?

―We should be especially careful of poison.

At first, they exchanged such telepathic messages every time. Gradually, however, the frequency decreased, until now they were sending each other messages wondering what dish they would be eating that day.

And today, a cry of joy burst from Geom Mugeuk’s lips.

“We’ve finally returned!”

The carriage had arrived at Maga Village.

“This is where Maga Village begins. Shall we walk from here while looking around?”

Han Seol grew tense. She had heard rumors about Maga Village since she was young. There was a village where demonic practitioners lived in front of the main headquarters of the Demonic Cult, and its name was Maga Village.

They said no one who entered the village ever came out in one piece.

Some said that one person had emerged with both arms cut off, another as a madman. They said the villagers made dumplings out of human flesh, and sold dolls of the cursed Demon Venerables. All sorts of terrifying rumors abounded there.

People with sinister gazes would be coming and going along the streets, while drunken men leaned against the walls and cast gloomy looks her way. Of course, with the Young Cult Master beside her, no one would dare openly provoke her, would they?

―No matter what happens, please endure it as much as possible!

Han Seol sent this message to the Hanbing Twin Swords before following Geom Mugeuk out of the carriage. At last, she took her first step onto the grounds before the Demonic Cult.

Chapter 558: Buy Them All and I'll Give You 30% Off!
----------------------------------------------------
“Is this Maga Village?”

At the sight spread before her, Han Seol opened her eyes wide.

It was not the scene she had expected.

Clean streets lined with shops on both sides, and countless pedestrians were coming and going.

Neither those with wicked eyes seeking a quick score, nor those staggering drunk, could be found. In the alleyways where schemers would normally put their heads together, children were running and playing.

To her question of whether this was truly Maga Village, Geom Mugeuk had no need to answer.

“Young Cult Leader!”

The medicinal herb merchant at the entrance ran out and greeted Geom Mugeuk with a bright smile.

“Is business going well?”

“Thanks to you, it is.”

Honestly, Han Seol was surprised. He greeted the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult so casually? A mere street merchant?

Of course, she did not know. That in Maga Village, Geom Mugeuk’s popularity was even higher than the Heavenly Demon’s. That this place had become such a pleasant village to live in was all thanks to Geom Mugeuk’s efforts.

Among the Frigid Ice Twin Swords, Sobing quickly sent a telepathic message to Han Seol.

—Step back! They might be poisonous herbs.

—Surely they wouldn’t display and sell poisonous herbs like ordinary medicinal ingredients?

—That’s exactly why it’s the Demonic Cult.

Their hearts had been considerably relaxed during their culinary journey with Geom Mugeuk, but now that they had arrived at Maga Village, they tensed up again.

They were on edge, vigilant against even the slightest chance of someone targeting Han Seol.

“Young Cult Leader, it’s been a while!”

The merchant selling farming tools across the street also came out to greet Geom Mugik.

—Be careful. There might be concealed weapons disguised as farming tools.

They seemed ready to warn her to be careful of even the pebbles on the street from now on.

Then, Sahan, the husband of the Frigid Ice Twin Swords, sent a telepathic message to Han Seol.

—This street is staged because the Young Palace Lord is coming. They must have sent a letter in advance to arrange it.

Han Seol examined the merchants’ expressions and replied via telepathy.

—But these merchants seem genuinely happy to see the Young Cult Leader.

—They’re acting.

The Frigid Ice Twin Swords trusted Geom Mugeuk, but not the demonic followers.

They acknowledged that Geom Mugeuk had greatly helped the Northern Sea Ice Palace and that he possessed a disposition unbefitting a demonic follower.

But there was the Demonic Cult they knew. The one so savage and ruthless that even the fierce Sado Alliance had to back down.

If a single person does not change easily, would such a massive organization change easily?

So Sahan surmised:

—This place must be the first gate guarding the Demonic Cult’s main headquarters.

—A gate?

—Surely demonic followers are hidden everywhere. If a suspicious person appears, word will immediately reach the main headquarters.

Han Seol looked at Geom Mugeuk. The Geom Mugeuk she had experienced so far was not someone who would act friendly with merchants to deceive her. If it were such a place, he would have passed by without even getting off the carriage.

Still, she understood the Frigid Ice Twin Swords’ suspicion. Even she was greatly surprised by this unexpected atmosphere.

‘Even in front of the Murim Alliance, the atmosphere wouldn’t be this bright, would it? Is this really a staged street? Would they mobilize so many people to deceive us?’

Walking after Geom Mugeuk, she saw a shop selling dumplings. At the sight of steaming dumplings, she recalled the rumors about Maga Village.

‘Is there really human flesh inside those dumplings?’

Yet the middle-aged owner’s face was too kind, and the dumpling shop was packed with customers.

As she stared at the dumplings, Geom Mugeuk offered her some.

“They’re delicious here. Would you like to try some?”

“I’m fine.”

Fearing he might insist she eat some, Han Seol walked faster.

Those choosing clothes at the fabric store, and the owners and clerks of the fruit and grain shops, all recognized Geom Mugeuk and greeted him respectfully.

“May you sell well!”

Each time, Geom Mugeuk reciprocated their greetings one by one.

That sight struck Han Seol in a new light.

She had known Geom Mugeuk was a meddlesome person, but she had thought it was merely a kind of tactic deployed when dealing with her mother, the Ice Palace Lord, herself, or the successors of the Murim Alliance and Sado Alliance. Naturally, there must be hidden intentions behind that kindness. It was impressive because it was something ordinary people could not do even if they knew how.

But Geom Mugeuk treated the merchants exactly the same way.

‘How can this be?’

How could his manner toward the Young Palace Lord of the Northern Sea Ice Palace or other successors be the same as his manner toward shopkeepers? No, rather, he felt even more affectionate toward the merchants.

‘Is it really a disguised street, as the two elders said?’

What else could make her harbor such doubts?

Then, a peddler selling wooden dolls caught Han Seol’s eye.

‘They really exist! Someone selling Demon Lord dolls!’

He really had laid out a display stand and was selling Demon Lord dolls. When she had heard the rumors, she thought they were just scary-looking dolls called Demon Lord dolls.

They were truly modeled after the Demon Lords’ appearances. Having met Chwima in person, she could tell they looked exactly alike.

‘They even have a Heavenly Demon doll!’

She thought it was truly absurd. Selling a Murim Alliance Leader doll in front of the Murim Alliance headquarters would get you dragged away.

The goat-bearded merchant selling the dolls hawked to her.

“These are sold only here in the Central Plains! If you buy all the Demon Lords, I’ll give you a thirty percent discount!”

Then the peddler recognized Geom Mugeuk behind her.

“Ah, Young Cult Leader!”

“Have you been well?”

Han Seol saw the merchant’s expression brighten brilliantly.

‘That expression is acting?’

If so, then all the actors of the Central Plains must be gathered here.

Han Seol asked Geom Mugeuk.

“Did the Cult Leader really give permission to sell these?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Just by looking at this alone, you can see how broad my father’s magnanimity is.”

Sahan’s telepathic message flew in again.

—There is no way that is true. They might be cursed dolls, as the rumors say. In an emergency, those dolls might move on their own and attack the opponent.

Han Seol sent him a telepathic message.

—They are too cool and cute to be such dolls.

Because she thought they would look nice displayed on a shelf.

The goat-bearded merchant conveyed welcome news to Geom Mugeuk.

“I finally see you, Young Cult Leader. I was just meaning to tell you something.”

“What is it?”

“Your doll will be coming out soon too.”

“Oh! My doll is finally coming out too!”

Now that he had officially become the successor, they were planning to release a Geom Mugeuk doll as well.

“Do you happen to have a desired pose? I could not ask the others and made them arbitrarily, but I wanted to ask you before making yours.”

Han Seol could feel it. The very fact that he asked such a question showed how much the merchants here trusted Geom Mugeuk. How could anyone dare ask such a thing?

“Make it however you like. It just needs to be cool. Placed right there, it should be the first thing to reach for! You know?”

Geom Mugeuk lowered his voice deliberately, speaking as if whispering.

“Make it cooler than my father’s.”

The goat-bearded merchant smiled and nodded.

“Even if we make yours the worst, it will sell the most, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to Han Seol and said.

“Would you like to buy a doll? Which one do you like?”

Then, surprising words flowed from Han Seol’s mouth.

“I’ll take them all.”

At the same time, the Frigid Ice Twin Swords sent simultaneous telepathic messages.

—You must not!

—It is dangerous!

Of course, if she were someone who listened to them, she would not have come here in the first place.

“You said you would give a thirty percent discount if I buy them all?”

At her words, the goat-bearded merchant began packaging them and said.

“Excellent choice! Display them and your home will truly shine brightly. Guests will all ask where you bought them.”

Seeing the Frigid Ice Twin Swords still shaking their heads, she smiled and said.

“We came to visit the Divine Cult, so should we not buy at least one souvenir?”

Items from other shops could be found anywhere. But these dolls were said to be sold only in Maga Village. And above all, she felt like buying them.

Then Sahan stepped forward.

“Before that, let us examine them.”

Sahan carefully examined the bundled dolls. No matter how he looked, they were ordinary dolls.

“These will do.”

The goat-bearded merchant began packaging again, and Geom Mugeuk asked her.

“Which one do you like best? You have seen Chwima before, so would he not feel the most familiar?”

But unexpectedly, she chose the Gwonma doll. She chose the one that was the biggest and scariest-looking among the dolls.

Geom Mugeuk smiled at her taste.

“I see our young lady likes scary faces. Ah, so it was also true that you like men of few words.”

Among the Eight Demon Lords, Gwonma was second to none when it came to being taciturn.

Meanwhile, the goat-bearded merchant wrapped them up well and handed them to her. Though Geom Mugeuk tried to buy them as a gift, she paid with her own money.

“Please patronize us again when the Young Cult Leader doll comes out!”

“I wonder if I will ever come back.”

At her words, Geom Mugeuk quickly said.

“Where is there a stronger reason than this?”

“You are telling me to come all the way here to buy a doll?”

“Why would only my doll be made? Yours might be made too, and perhaps even a Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord doll.”

Yes, this was someone who would even tell that goat-bearded peddler to make a doll of himself.

Would his pride not be hurt being sold alongside them? Would his honor not be tarnished?

Geom Mugeuk was not someone who would think that at all. Probably neither were the Heavenly Demon nor the Demon Lords. That must be why they kept selling like this.

It was she who thought that way. Because she would have hated being sold alongside dolls that did not match her level.

Through these dolls, she could look back at herself. She realized she possessed such pretension and useless authoritarianism that even those far stronger than her did not have.

‘They really must be cursed dolls. Seeing how they stab my heart like this.’

She had lived without ever caring about others. But when she was with this person, she kept being pulled out of her own solitary world.

Then, someone ran out shouting from the tavern across the street.

“Young Cult Leader!”

He was happier to see Geom Mugeuk than any merchant they had encountered so far.

He was none other than Jo Chunbae, the owner of Pungnyu Tavern. Having heard through customers that the Young Cult Leader was walking through Maga Village, he had come running out.

“Do not fall, come slowly.”

“Young Cult Leader! Have you come?”

Jo Chunbae was truly overjoyed.

She glanced at the Frigid Ice Twin Swords. No more telepathic messages claiming this was all an act came from them.

“Owner, have you been well?”

“Of course, of course. I have been well. Are you hurt anywhere?”

“I am fine.”

“Thank goodness, truly thank goodness.”

This departure from the cult had been quite long, so Jo Chunbae had been inwardly worried about Geom Mugeuk.

“You seem to have lost more weight, owner.”

Geom Mugeuk took his hand. Jo Chunbae exclaimed, “Oh my, please do not,” flustered.

Geom Mugeuk introduced Jo Chunbae to Han Seol.

“This is the owner of my regular tavern.”

In her life as the successor of the Ice Palace, she had never been introduced to a tavern owner, nor would she ever be again.

“This is the Young Palace Lord of the Northern Sea Ice Palace.”

At the words “Northern Sea Ice Palace,” Jo Chunbae jumped in surprise. The Northern Sea Ice Palace held a different meaning than the Murim Alliance or Sado Alliance. To drinkers, the Northern Sea Ice Palace was the most mysterious sect.

“Oh my, it is an honor to meet such a noble person.”

Jo Chunbae bowed his head respectfully.

“Owner, I must pay my respects to my father. I will see you later.”

“Please come anytime!”

As they were turning to leave, Jo Chunbae spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

“You have really returned safely, Young Cult Leader.”

Han Seol could feel it. That he was as happy as if his own son had returned.

Han Seol sent a telepathic message to the Frigid Ice Twin Swords as if declaring:

—Even after this?

But the Frigid Ice Twin Swords could not believe it until the end.

—They said the trilateral meeting was held at a tavern in Maga Village. Would such an important event be held at an ordinary tavern?

They still believed that some secret unknown to them was hidden here in Maga Village.

“Later, let us have a drink with friends there. There are also friends in the main cult I wish to introduce you to.”

To her, who had not a single friend, friends were truly pouring in.

The four who had left Maga Village set out toward the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“How is it? Your impression after visiting Maga Village?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Han Seol answered exactly as she felt.

“It was nice. Much brighter and cleaner than I’d imagined.”

Just then, Sahan cut in and threw out a pointed remark.

“As if it had been deliberately made that way.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“It was deliberately made that way.”

For a moment, the startled gazes of the three turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“At first, it wasn’t like this. It was so rough that anyone could tell at a glance it was Maga Village. There were plenty of bastards throwing their weight around just because they were martial artists, and plenty who got drunk and caused trouble. When I became the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master, I locked them all up. The ones who drank and beat people, the ones who stole money or threatened others, the ones who swindled and killed people—I caught and punished them all. If they caused trouble while drunk, I doubled the usual sentence. If a martial artist laid a hand on ordinary people, I punished them three times as severely. The truth is, this place was made.”

Han Seol stared silently at Geom Mugeuk, then asked calmly.

“Why did you go that far?”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk spoke.

“Because it was something I could do. No—more precisely, because it was something I could do easily.”

Han Seol could feel it from the look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes as he gazed at her. He meant it sincerely.

“It’s something you and I can do with ease. We’re in positions free from all sorts of conflicting interests. If we have the will, it can be done.”

Han Seol said nothing for a while. He was a successor, and so was she. Yet she had not once known what was happening in the villages near the North Sea Ice Palace. Because she had never once cared. Perhaps the truly cursed dolls were being sold there.

She had merely walked through the marketplace, and yet her thoughts had grown heavy.

In the meantime, they arrived at the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

The fortress walls were so high they seemed to pierce the sky, and on the enormous iron gate were carved the forms of evil spirits.

Han Seol grew tense. She had finally come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, a place she had only heard about since childhood. This was a place even her mother, the Ice Palace Mistress, had never visited, and she had come here first. It was also the first time the Hanbing Twin Swords had been to the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

The elite demonic practitioners guarding the main gate respectfully bowed to Geom Mugeuk.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader!”

The thunderous shout rang out in all directions. The gazes of the demonic practitioners were sharp, and their discipline was clearly firm. At the sight, the Hanbing Twin Swords exchanged looks, each telling the other to be even more cautious.

Grrrrrrng.

Ordinary demonic practitioners came and went through a small door beneath the massive iron gate, but as the Young Cult Leader returned from his long time away from the cult, the enormous iron gate began to open.

At the scene revealed beyond it, Han Seol and the Hanbing Twin Swords cried out in awe at the same time.

“Ah!”

The interior was so vast that its end could not be seen, and the buildings erected throughout were tall and magnificent. The North Sea Ice Palace was also large and imposing, but it could not compare to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

A broad, expansive avenue led toward its center. On either side of that road stood rows of gigantic statues of evil spirits.

Set up like gatekeepers, they looked down arrogantly with terrifying eyes, as if to say that once one passed through here, there would be no turning back.

Han Seol was overwhelmed by the majesty of the main headquarters, and she had never seen stone statues so enormous before.

Geom Mugeuk stood before them, smiled, and said,

“Welcome to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Chapter 559: More Handsome Than a Doll!
---------------------------------------
Han Seol followed Geom Mugeuk.

The martial artists passing by saw Geom Mugeuk and greeted him with polite courtesy.

She could feel it in their eyes. Deep trust and loyalty toward Geom Mugeuk.

Was it because of what happened at Magachon? She kept comparing herself to him.

She had never before paid attention to how the Ice Palace martial artists looked at her. No, she had probably seen it before, but because she had no interest, she could not remember. For the first time, she became curious about what kind of gazes they must hold.

They entered the Cheonma Hall.

Han Seol’s heart pounded at the thought of meeting the Cult Leader for the first time.

Then Sa Han’s voice transmission flew in.

—Do not be nervous! We will protect you.

Actually, the Hanbing Twin Swords were more nervous. They had thought they would never see the Heavenly Demon in their lives.

Far away, they saw the figure of Geom Ujin sitting in a grand chair.

Han Seol felt that his figure, alone in this vast Cheonma Hall, was both unfamiliar and lonely.

Walking down the scarlet path, Geom Mugeuk spoke loudly.

“Father! Your son has returned after completing his mission.”

His voice resounded mightily.

Though he must have thought no one could see from so far away, Geom Mugeuk saw with his Divine Eye Technique that a faint smile formed at his father’s lips. He must be rejoicing at his son’s return.

Having arrived beneath the grand chair, Geom Mugeuk looked up at his father. Their gazes met.

“Seeing you, Father, I finally feel as though I have returned to our headquarters.”

To feel this sense of relief just from seeing my father. Truly, thinking back to when I first regressed, this is something I could never have imagined.

Han Seol was looking up at Geom Ujin wordlessly.

Geom Ujin’s gaze turned toward her.

‘He’s even more handsome than the doll!’

The handsomeness and presence of the real Geom Ujin were absolutely beyond what any doll could capture.

I thought they had beautified the doll because he is the ‘Heavenly Demon,’ but the real thing was far more handsome. I had expected a terribly fearsome appearance.

And only when she came close and saw him could she feel it. That this space was not vast at all. The presence of the man called Geom Ujin filled the entire space.

Geom Mugeuk introduced Han Seol.

“She is the Young Palace Lady of the Northern Sea Ice Palace.”

Of course, since he had sent word to the Tongcheon Pavilion in advance, he must have known that Han Seol would be returning with him.

Han Seol greeted him with polite courtesy.

“I am Han Seol. It is a great honor to meet such a venerable person.”

“You must have had a hard journey.”

“Thanks to the Young Cult Lord, I was able to enjoy sightseeing in the Central Plains.”

The Hanbing Twin Swords who had come with her also greeted him politely.

“We are the Hanbing Twin Swords. We greet the Supreme of the Divine Cult.”

“I have heard much of your fame.”

The two felt something far more intense than Han Seol had.

The moment their eyes met Geom Ujin’s, they felt their whole bodies freeze. Even though he was not emitting any particular killing intent, Geom Ujin’s presence overwhelmed the two of them.

The moment Sa Han tried to oppose that presence.

Swoosh.

Slash.

With the sound of a blade wind came the sound of flesh being cut.

Sa Han looked down at his chest. It was drenched in blood. A long line of blood was drawn across So Bing’s chest beside him as well.

Sa Han looked behind him.

He saw the retreating figure of Geom Ujin, who had cut past him and So Bing.

Sa Han looked down at his own sword. It was drawn about a hand’s breadth.

‘This is the difference in skill between the Heavenly Demon and me!’

All of this had happened inside Sa Han’s imagination.

He liked to read his opponent’s presence or intent and imagine and predict his own battle, and indeed, most fights ended just as he predicted.

If he had tried to fight the Heavenly Demon today? The result would not have been much different.

‘An instant defeat!’

He knew the Cult Leader was strong, but never had he imagined his presence would be at this level.

Geom Ujin spoke to the three of them.

“Rest comfortably at our headquarters before you go.”

Han Seol and the Hanbing Twin Swords cupped their fists and bowed.

“Thank you.”

Geom Ujin said to Geom Mugeuk.

“Treat them well.”

“Yes, then I will come to see you again later.”

He had much to tell his father, but that would have to wait for a time when just the two of them could be together.

After Han Seol and the Hanbing Twin Swords bowed to Geom Ujin, they exited Cheonma Hall together with Geom Mugeuk.

“Haah.”

As soon as they opened the door and came out, Han Seol let out a sigh. She had merely gone in to greet him and come out, yet her whole body ached as if she had caught a cold. It was from being too tense.

Sa Han’s expression was stiff. Seeing that, So Bing could guess what sort of imagination her husband had had and how it had turned out.

“How was my father?”

To Geom Mugeuk’s question, Han Seol answered honestly.

“He was truly magnificent.”

“Even you, please don’t say that. I have been losing to my father in popularity contests lately.”

“He deserves it.”

“How cruel!”

Geom Mugeuk guided them to the guest hall where the most honored guests stayed. It was decorated so splendidly that one might wonder if a room for greeting guests should really be this lavish.

“You must be tired from the long journey, so rest well first. Let’s meet at the Pungnyu Tavern this evening.”

“Thank you.”

Just then, a subordinate came to report. Before going to Cheonma Hall, Geom Mugeuk had ordered a subordinate to find out if Ian had returned.

“This martial artist has not yet returned.”

A look of regret flashed across Han Seol’s face upon hearing the report.

“I might leave without seeing my older sister.”

This too was an unfamiliar regret. She had never thought she would feel disappointed at not being able to meet someone.

Then the subordinate added.

“Oh, and the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord has just returned.”

“Did he come alone?”

Geom Mugeuk knew that Seo Daeryong’s fate would change depending on the answer to this question.

“No. He came with women.”

Geom Mugeuk’s face brightened brilliantly.

After the subordinate finished reporting and withdrew, Han Seol asked Geom Mugeuk.

“What has you so happy?”

“It seems my right-hand man has finally met his spring.”

She made an expression showing she could not understand what he meant, but instead of explaining, Geom Mugeuk made an unexpected proposal.

“How about staying at our headquarters until Ian returns?”

Taken aback by the unexpected words, not only Han Seol but also the Hanbing Twin Swords were surprised.

“Is there any reason you must hurry back to the Northern Sea?”

“There isn’t.”

“Then there is nothing wrong with staying, is there?”

“Is it not strange? The Young Palace Lady of the Northern Sea Ice Palace staying at the Divine Cult.”

“Just think of it as visiting a friend’s house.”

He had used the expression ‘friend’ at Cheonhwaru too, and now he was calling her a friend again.

“Do you truly consider me a friend?”

It was a cautious question, but the answer came easily.

“I do.”

“We do not know much about each other, do we?”

“Where is the friend who starts by knowing everything? If the feeling is right, you become friends. Rather, the more you know, the harder it becomes to be friends.”

Han Seol could not answer. She had never had a friend before.

“Think about it. If we were to become friends when we are older, we would scrutinize everything. What we did in the past, what kind of personality we have, what we are like when drunk, whether we hold grudges, how greedy we are. If even one thing does not sit right, it would feel so off that we could be friends no longer. But when we are young, is it not the opposite? Even if you hate everything else, as long as one thing clicks, you can become friends.”

“Then what clicked with me?”

Then words she had never expected flowed out.

“That you are a refreshing person, like the Northern Sea wind.”

Han Seol’s eyes widened slightly.

“‘Let’s go to the main compound,’ and you go. ‘Let’s meet at the Sadomaeng,’ and you go. If we buy dolls, you buy them all. I think it would be nice to have a friend who lives on instinct. Because I am someone with many worries and thoughts. Now then, rest comfortably, and see you later.”

As Geom Mugeuk left the guest hall, Han Seol stood blankly for a moment.

That we clicked because I am refreshing like the wind?

That I am a friend who lives on instinct?

It was the first time in her life she had heard such words. And it was not unpleasant.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk’s proposal did not sit well with the Hanbing Twin Swords.

“You must not stay long at the Divine Cult.”

Han Seol asked him back.

“Are you afraid the Young Cult Lord is scheming something?”

“Even if not, this place is the most dangerous dragon’s pond and tiger’s den in the martial world. We do not know what will happen from now on.”

Han Seol walked to the window. She saw the retreating figure of Geom Mugeuk walking out past the gardens of the guest hall. Now that she thought about it, she had really come all the way here trusting only that one person.

“In the mountain of blades and forest of swords that is the Central Plains martial world, is this not precisely the place where we can rest for a moment?”

At those words, the Hanbing Twin Swords looked at each other. These days, the Han Seol they had watched since she was young felt unfamiliar. She was someone who, if the cold wind blew fiercely and made her even a little uncomfortable, would get up and leave.

Sa Han spoke to her.

“Do not trust the Young Cult Leader too much. He is, after all, a demonic martial artist. No, before that, do not trust people.”

Han Seol turned to the two and asked.

“But you two trust each other, do you not?”

“Well, that is…”

Just as Sa Han was trying to persuade her, So Bing spoke.

“For however long it is, at least enjoy yourself while you are here.”

Sa Han looked at So Bing.

—Is this not the Young Palace Lady’s first time in the Central Plains?

After sending that voice transmission to her husband, So Bing spoke in a calm tone. It was not necessarily directed at Han Seol.

“When else would we ever come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult?”

* * *

“From now on, is this where we will live?”

The youngest, Danyeon, asked in a trembling voice at the majesty of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s main compound.

Dana pretended to be fine, but she was trembling just the same.

She looked at Seo Daeryong beside her. She had come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s main compound trusting only this man. And she had brought her two younger sisters with her.

Seo Daeryong looked at Dana and said.

“From now on, this is home. Let’s go in.”

Seo Daeryong entered through the main gate with them.

Those guarding the main gate were fearsome demonic martial artists with whom it was hard to even make eye contact.

They bowed courteously to Seo Daeryong.

“Oh, Brother-in-law! So cool?”

At Danyeon's whisper, Seo Daeryong put strength into his shoulders with a proud expression.

When they entered through a small gate.

“Woooah!”

Danyeon shouted without realizing it. She was startled by the giant demon statue.

“It looks so scary.”

“Scary? It’s going to protect us now.”

Hearing those words, the Dan sisters realized their fate had changed.

The three women walked to the inner courtyard, constantly looking around in admiration. The scale was truly overwhelming.

Among those passing by, demonic martial artists who recognized the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord politely bowed their heads.

Seeing people mostly greeting him first, the Dan sisters once again truly felt Seo Daeryong’s importance.

They had vaguely known he was a high-ranking person, but this was the moment they confirmed with their own eyes that he was truly a high-ranking figure of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Seo Daeryong first brought them to his residence.

Because maidservants had thoroughly cleaned, the house was clean despite him being away for a long time.

“Stay here for the time being.”

Since it was the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord’s residence, there were several rooms.

“Sister-in-law, pick whichever room you like!”

By now, the word ‘sister-in-law’ rolled off Seo Daeryong’s tongue. Of course, he only called the youngest one ‘sister-in-law’ because she took his jokes so well.

“The house is amazing!”

Danyeon, who was running from room to room in joy, said to her second older sister.

“I’m giving you first pick. Which room do you want?”

She knew that her second older sister wanted to settle down more than anyone, so she had let her choose first.

Lying on the bed in the room she had chosen, as she looked out the window, a sense of relief crossed Danbi’s face.

She had stayed in far better places, but no matter how nice they were, they were not home. Now this place was her home and her room.

Seeing Danbi like that, Danyeon smiled. Actually, she was not as eager to settle down as her older sister. She still preferred traveling around the Central Plains, but she did not show that for her sisters’ sake.

After choosing rooms, the three sisters gathered in one place.

Dana lowered her head to Seo Daeryong to express her gratitude.

“Thank you.”

“What do you mean? I am the one thankful that you trusted me and followed me here.”

Dana had made a resolution on the way here.

“We will not be a burden to the Pavilion Lord. You do not need to overexert yourself worrying about us. We can start from the lowest-ranking martial artist. We will take the exam and join officially.”

She had heard that the Hwangcheon Pavilion was an organization that enforced the laws of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. She was worried that if he used his authority as Pavilion Lord to place them somewhere, it would cause problems.

She was confident they could survive on their own. They were women who had overcome even the difficult life of wanderers.

“Don’t worry about us, Brother-in-law! If it really doesn’t work out, we can do chores.”

Then someone spoke.

“We cannot have precious guests doing such things.”

The four turned around in surprise to see Geom Mugeuk standing in the doorway.

“Young Cult Lord!”

The Dan sisters hurriedly bowed.

“We greet the Young Cult Lord.”

They were acquainted with Geom Mugeuk, having seen him at the inn and at the branch office.

Seo Daeryong rushed to Geom Mugeuk.

“Are you hurt anywhere?”

“I am fine.”

Seo Daeryong spoke, seeing that he was still fussing over him in front of Dana.

“You may speak comfortably now. We are going to be seeing a lot of each other from now on.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly at them and said.

“Welcome.”

“We will serve you with full loyalty going forward.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Seo Daeryong. Seo Daeryong smiled sheepishly but provoked him with the expression of a victor. For the first time in a while, Geom Mugeuk played along with his antics.

“Daring to fall in love before the Young Cult Lord?”

“I am a bit older, after all.”

The two looked at each other and laughed.

Geom Mugeuk said to Dana.

“You have excellent taste in men.”

Dana smiled, and Seo Daeryong waved his hands telling him not to say such things. But Seo Daeryong’s eyes said, ‘Please continue a little more.’

“You once said to me, Pavilion Lord Seo, that the headquarters would never change. The change at headquarters began the moment you spoke those words to me. Perhaps Pavilion Lord Seo wanted to bring the three of you to the headquarters.”

The three women did not quite understand what he meant, but Seo Daeryong’s heart welled up.

Geom Mugeuk said to the three women.

“If it had not been for you all this time, things would have gotten truly dangerous.”

After all, it would have led to the death of the Cheonhwaru Lord.

“It is unthinkable for those who have rendered such great merit to start as low-level martial artists. I intend to prepare positions for you all.”

As if he had a place in mind for each of them, Geom Mugeuk spoke to the three women.

“You have been together your whole lives, so how about practicing being apart now?”

Chapter 560: The Wind Has Already Blown
---------------------------------------
The Dan sisters were startled and flustered.

They truly had never expected the Young Cult Master to personally arrange positions for them.

Dana looked at Seo Daeryong with a dazed expression.

His face was filled with emotion as he expressed his gratitude to Geom Mugeuk.

“Young Cult Master, thank you.”

It was all thanks to Geom Mugeuk that he had been able to come this far. And now, Geom Mugeuk was willing to step forward for the woman he loved—and even for her younger sisters.

“Don’t get moved just yet! You might only be asked to shovel horse dung in the stables!”

Dana answered energetically.

“If it is something the Young Cult Master commands, I will do anything. Even if you told me to clear corpses every day instead of horse dung, I would do it.”

Her two sisters shouted together.

“Just leave it to us.”

They were sincere. They were already deeply grateful simply because the Young Cult Master of the Demonic Heavenly Cult was personally looking after them.

“Then follow me.”

The first place Geom Mugeuk headed was the Ghost Shadow Unit, led by Ian.

The First Squad Leader, Cheongmyeon, came out to greet him.

“Young Cult Master! I heard that you had returned.”

All the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Unit came out and paid their respects. Perhaps they had been devoted to their training during that time, for their presence was even sharper than when Geom Mugeuk had first seen them.

After examining them, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze returned to Cheongmyeon.

He did not ask how things had been going lately. Instead, he asked:

“Are you happy?”

Cheongmyeon had chosen the path of an ordinary squad leader instead of remaining the Extreme Evil Little Demon’s personal disciple.

His eyes, visible through the mask, smiled pleasantly.

“Yes.”

That brief answer was enough. Geom Mugeuk could feel that Cheongmyeon was satisfied with his life.

“I fought alongside the Little Demon this time.”

Geom Mugeuk deliberately mentioned the Extreme Evil Little Demon. Would avoiding the subject really make Cheongmyeon forget him? Rather, Geom Mugeuk believed that speaking naturally was the better thing to do for him.

“I survived thanks to the Little Demon.”

An unmistakable longing passed through Cheongmyeon’s eyes.

“Thank you for bringing me the news.”

After sharing that long-awaited reunion, Geom Mugeuk revealed the reason he had come today.

“Today, I’ve come to recommend a new recruit to the Ghost Shadow Unit.”

Since this was Geom Mugeuk’s first recommendation, Cheongmyeon looked surprised as he glanced at the three women who had come with him.

The person Geom Mugeuk had chosen was Danyeon.

“Youngest Miss!”

Danyeon was startled, never imagining that he would call for her.

“Me?”

“Youngest Miss, the Ghost Shadow Unit is my direct organization, and the place where my heart serves as its commander. It is also where Cheongmyeon, whom I trust, serves as First Squad Leader.”

“Are you trying to place me in such an important organization?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“I believe the Ghost Shadow Unit will suit you, given your lively personality. From now on, the Ghost Shadow Unit will travel throughout the Central Plains carrying out missions. What do you think? Would you like to give it a try?”

Danyeon was surprised that Geom Mugeuk had understood her personality so well.

Her gaze shifted from Geom Mugeuk to the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Unit. Cheongmyeon, wearing his blue mask, and the warriors standing behind him all possessed an extraordinary presence. She would not merely be a wandering martial artist anymore. She would become an elite warrior of the Demonic Cult.

“Can I really do it?”

“That is something neither I nor the First Squad Leader over there can know. Only you can know the answer. Youngest Miss, can you do it?”

For all the importance of the matter, she did not take long to decide. What stamped the seal on her decision was neither some vague hope that she wanted to do well nor some vague confidence that she could.

It was the path she had walked until now. The past she had overcome—the harsh life of a wandering martial artist—made the decision for her with a resounding bang.

Danyeon respectfully clasped her hands toward Cheongmyeon and bowed.

“If you will accept me, I will do my very best.”

Geom Mugeuk thought that the youngest was clever. Since he had brought her there, Cheongmyeon would have no choice but to accept her. Yet she was still asking for his permission.

“You have the Young Cult Master’s recommendation, but there will be no special treatment.”

“That is exactly what I want.”

Cheongmyeon accepted her with a pleased expression.

“Report here starting tomorrow.”

“Thank you!”

“Thank the Young Cult Master. We are shadows who exist solely for the Young Cult Master.”

Danyeon clasped her hands toward Geom Mugeuk and spoke energetically.

“Young Cult Master, thank you.”

Dana and Danbi bowed their heads at the same time.

“Thank you so much for looking after our sister.”

A mischievous glint crossed Geom Mugeuk’s face.

“It’s not because of you two, but because of our youngest miss. Now, Youngest Miss, will you still send dishes to my father?”

Danyeon smiled sheepishly, her expression making it clear that she could not give up that one thing.

“That’s too cruel! Youngest Miss!”
