Danyeon’s heart pounded. It did not feel real. Neither speaking so casually with the Young Cult Master of the Demonic Cult nor entering the Ghost Shadow Unit.

She had known that a new life would begin, but she had never imagined that everything would change so suddenly on her first day in the Demonic Heavenly Cult. She had not even finished unpacking her belongings.

How could Geom Mugeuk fail to understand her complicated feelings? He added some warm encouragement.

“The wind has already begun to blow, so our Youngest Miss must steer the sails well.”

* * *

“Thank you. You saved my life.”

Jong Hwa, an investigator of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, bowed respectfully.

While arresting a cult member who had committed corruption, the man had suddenly swung his sword and tried to take the investigator hostage, putting everyone in great danger.

Fortunately, the newly appointed Third Commander of the Execution Unit responded swiftly and saved his life.

“This is excessive force, you bastards! You’re all getting fired!”

The cult member, who had been stabbed by the sword during the process, began shouting. Terrified and drunk, he had no idea what he was doing.

“This is nothing. There will be all sorts of bastards from now on.”

At Jong Hwa’s words, the Third Commander nodded.

After Jong Hwa and the execution warriors arrested the criminal and departed first, the Third Commander was left alone and let out a sigh.

Then a voice came from behind him.

“Do you already miss the Jeongan Branch?”

He spun around in surprise and saw Geom Mugeuk walking toward him with Seo Daeryong and the Dan sisters.

“Young Cult Master?”

“It has been a while, Branch Leader Kang. No—Third Commander Kang.”

He was Kang Dal, the Jeongan Branch Leader of the Demonic Heavenly Cult. After making a great contribution by saving the Dan sisters and catching the attention of Geom Wujin, he had been appointed Third Commander of the Execution Unit.

There were three commanders under the Supreme Commander, and he had been appointed commander of the Third Unit. Being transferred from a regional branch to command an execution unit of the Yellow Springs Pavilion was an unprecedented promotion. It was a reward from Geom Wujin, who had recognized his judgment and ability to handle affairs.

He had been filled with worry when he was appointed and came to the main headquarters. Extraordinary appointments always brought gossip and dissatisfaction.

But this time, not a single word of gossip or complaint arose.

On the day of his appointment, Geom Wujin had personally visited the Yellow Springs Pavilion. In front of all the execution warriors, he praised Kang Dal for his great contribution and personally appointed him commander.

Kang Dal would never forget the sight of the Cult Leader walking through the open doors into the place where he had been assigned, not until the day he died.

After respectfully greeting Geom Mugeuk, Kang Dal formally paid his respects to Seo Daeryong as well.

“I will serve you with complete loyalty from now on.”

Seo Daeryong was now his direct superior.

Although this was the first time they had met in person, Seo Daeryong had already received the report of Kang Dal’s appointment as an Execution Commander.

“I believe that you will perform admirably, Commander Kang.”

“I will do my utmost not to disappoint you.”

When the two had finished exchanging greetings, Geom Mugeuk told Kang Dal why he had come.

“Please accept one new recruit into the Third Unit of the Execution Division.”

“As it happens, we were just about to replenish our personnel.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at his second sister, Danbi.

“The Second Miss is calm and rational, so I believe she is suited to being an execution warrior. What do you think? Would you like to try it?”

She had been prepared to do anything, from serving as a low-ranking warrior to handling menial chores. How could she dislike becoming an execution warrior of the Yellow Springs Pavilion?

“Thank you so much.”

Danbi’s martial arts ability was also sufficient for the position.

The Dan sisters were sincerely moved—not only by being entrusted with important positions, but also by Geom Mugeuk’s consideration in taking each of their personalities into account. In truth, that was the most important part.

What if the position the Young Cult Master arranged for them did not suit them? Since it was not something they could simply quit, it would have been truly painful.

Danbi respectfully greeted Kang Dal.

“I look forward to working with you, Commander!”

“It is even more pleasant since we have met before.”

Kang Dal smiled. In truth, he had risen so far because the Dan sisters had come to his branch.

The Young Cult Master, the Pavilion Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, and the Dan sisters.

Their connection continued even after coming to the main headquarters.

And so Geom Mugeuk had found positions for the second and third sisters.

“There is no position for the eldest sister.”

Naturally, there was a reason Geom Mugeuk had made this decision.

“First, I want the two of you to have plenty of time together.”

The two of them liked each other and had come all the way to the main headquarters together. But if Dana took on a new position, she would be busy and distracted adapting to the work.

“For the time being, take your time and enjoy yourselves. Go out together, buy yourselves delicious food, adjust to the Cult, and look after your sisters.”

Seo Daeryong’s entire body trembled at Geom Mugeuk’s thoughtfulness.

‘There is no way I can be more moved by the Young Cult Master. I’ve already used up all the emotion in my life on him, haven’t I? But how am I supposed to bear it if he keeps doing this?’

He truly felt that way. That was why the following question came out naturally.

“What are you going to do later?”

“Do what?”

“You’re already this handsome and impressive while you’re young. What will you do later?”

“If I end up shabby, boring, and unimpressive, you can tell people this: Our Young Cult Master used up all the handsomeness in his life during his twenties! So please understand him.”

Kang Dal and the Dan sisters laughed along with Seo Daeryong.

Geom Mugeuk sincerely hoped that Seo Daeryong and Dana would succeed. But he knew that it was not something he could make happen by interfering.

“If a life spent as a wandering martial artist has brought you all the way to the main headquarters alongside the Pavilion Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, then I believe you must have a destiny of your own. Follow wherever that destiny leads you.”

Dana bowed respectfully.

“I will never forget the grace you have shown us.”

Only now could she understand why Seo Daeryong spoke of the Young Cult Master whenever he opened his mouth. Geom Mugeuk was simply that kind of person.

“Then let’s all meet at the Pungnyu Tavern this evening! Commander Kang, you come too!”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk went straight to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Elder!”

When he ran into the courtyard, someone was already there.

“Young Cult Master, whenever you return from outside the Cult, this is always the first place you come.”

The woman tending the flower garden straightened her back. Her makeup-free face and pure white hair now suited her perfectly. She was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“I like you best, Elder. Have you been well, Sword Venerable?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled brightly and nodded. Her presence had grown even gentler, so it seemed her training had not stopped.

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon walked out of the storehouse and spoke.

“Lies, every word!”

Even so, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could not hide his pleased expression.

“Elder!”

Geom Mugeuk ran over and tried to embrace him, but as always, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon used his footwork to evade him.

Holding the hoe he had brought from the storehouse, he squatted down beside the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and began digging into the earth.

Geom Mugeuk watched the two of them tend the flower garden for a moment.

What did they feel toward each other?

There was at least one thing he could sense. They were not forcing themselves to draw closer. They simply looked at each other from exactly that distance.

Perhaps that distance was what protected the two of them.

As he worked the hoe, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked:

“How did it go?”

“I came back from the brink of death. If Vice Alliance Leader Bi and Commander of the Demon-Slaying Unit had not been there—and especially if the Little Demon had not been there—I would have died.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s hands stopped briefly, then began moving again.

“It’ll only get harder. The more of them you kill, the more viciously and powerfully they’ll strike back.”

“You have to protect me, Elder.”

“Don’t think about making an old man work for you. Just train hard.”

From now on, Geom Mugeuk intended to work truly hard. He was not the only one whose pride had been wounded when the Seven-Star Nine Fire Demon Art had failed to work.

“Sword Venerable, I think we need to restart our sparring partnership.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled.

“I’m always happy to.”

Just then, two people entered the courtyard.

They were Seo Daeryong and Dana. Since they had returned from outside the Cult, they had come to greet his master. Geom Mugeuk had brought Dana along so that he could introduce her as well.

“Master, I’ve returned.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon rose and walked toward Seo Daeryong.

His worried gaze swept over his disciple’s body.

“Are you hurt anywhere?”

“No, I’m fine.”

“Then that’s enough.”

Geom Mugeuk was not the sort of person who would simply watch this happen.

“No, even if he is your first disciple, aren’t you showing him too much favoritism? I was in greater danger!”

As though he had not heard him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon turned his gaze toward Dana.

“Who is this young lady?”

“She is Miss Dana. She came to the main headquarters with me this time.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at Seo Daeryong with an expression that asked what he meant. Seo Daeryong did not know how to explain it and merely flushed red.

Dana was nervous. She truly wanted to make a good impression on Seo Daeryong’s master, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Her heart was pounding even harder than when she had been surrounded by enemies.

At that moment, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable rose and walked toward them.

“Why even ask?”

She looked at Seo Daeryong and Dana. Seeing the fresh, budding affection between two people who could barely even look each other in the eye, she smiled naturally.

‘I was like that once, too.’

Seo Daeryong introduced the Ilhwa Sword Venerable to Dana.

“She is the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, one of the Martial Venerables of our Cult.”

Dana stared at her in surprise. There was no one who wielded a sword who did not know her.

The woman who had risen to the rank of Martial Venerable with a single sword.

To Dana, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was a particularly special person. Even after coming here, she had been the Martial Venerable she most wanted to meet after the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, Seo Daeryong’s master.

“It is an honor to meet you.”

Dana’s voice trembled. Her admiration for the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was plainly visible on her face, and so her first impression passed with flying colors.

“It couldn’t have been easy for a woman to resolve to devote herself to our Cult.”

Perhaps it was because Dana was in love with Seo Daeryong, who wore a greatsword at his waist. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable was reminded of her own youth.

“What do you like about him so much?”

Since it was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable herself who had asked, Dana answered honestly.

“I like his pure nature, which is unlike that of a martial artist.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable nodded and turned back toward the flower garden. Then, at the sight of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and Seo Daeryong standing side by side with greatswords strapped to their backs, she suddenly turned around and asked:

“Could it be that Pavilion Lord Seo…”

Had she guessed what the question would be? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon quickly cut her off.

“Let’s not stand around here. We should go inside. I happen to have some good tea.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon took Dana inside, followed by Seo Daeryong.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled as she recalled that distant day.

“Just like master, just like disciple!”

Chapter 561: I Want to Live Like This Person
--------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk silently watched Blood Heaven Blade Demon brewing tea.

Until now, he had brewed tea many times, but today it felt different from usual.

"Let me do it."

Unable to simply sit still, Dana offered to take his place.

"It's fine."

He spoke gruffly, but Blood Heaven Blade Demon's gaze toward Dana was gentle.

Dana assisted Blood Heaven Blade Demon from the side. She tried hard to make a good impression on him. Having lived as a wanderer, she couldn't afford to be called discourteous.

"You lived as a wanderer with your siblings?"

"Yes."

"You must have had a hard time."

Those words sounded unfamiliar to Dana. She had never heard such things from an adult before. Everyone around her, children and adults alike, had been people desperately trying to survive.

"If that lad gives you any trouble, come tell me anytime."

Geom Mugeuk could tell. What kind of mood Blood Heaven Blade Demon was in today.

To him, today was the day his son brought the woman who would become his daughter-in-law.

Unlike Seo Daeryong, who was fidgeting restlessly a short distance away from the two, One Flower Sword Venerable silently watched Blood Heaven Blade Demon's back.

What could she be thinking now?

Was she recalling the young Blood Heaven Blade Demon who used to recite poems to her? Or was she recalling herself listening to those poems?

Those days when her father roamed the Central Plains with Fist Demon, when everyone had been young.

As One Flower Sword Venerable turned her head toward the window, her eyes met Geom Mugeuk's.

"Young Cult Leader."

"Yes, Sword Venerable."

"Now that it has passed, time is truly fleeting."

"Do you not have more days remaining?"

"Is that truly so?"

"You might even achieve returning to youth."

One Flower Sword Venerable shook her head with a tutting expression.

"Do not think it is over. In about ten years, you may look back on today and regret, thinking you were hale and hearty back then, that you should have done such and such."

She looked out the window again. With those deepened eyes, it was impossible to know what she was thinking.

Meanwhile, Blood Heaven Blade Demon and Dana brought the tea.

"The aroma is excellent."

Geom Mugeuk savored the fragrance, taking in the scent.

The first to exclaim in admiration was Dana.

"It's so delicious. It's the best tea I've ever tasted."

At that, Geom Mugeuk said to her,

"Of course. This is a tea he said he'd serve only to precious guests, never to us."

Dana took it as a joke, but Seo Daeryong nodded with a genuine expression.

Joining in, One Flower Sword Venerable said,

"This is my first time drinking this tea as well."

Naturally, Dana's face turned red.

She was truly happy. Until now, most of the joy she had felt came from the relief of having survived. This commission finished safely. Her siblings were safe.

But the joy she felt now was different from that.

She was drinking tea that the man she liked's master had specially prepared for her. Before her was One Flower Sword Venerable, whom she had always admired, and the Young Cult Leader who had saved her and her siblings. And above all, Seo Daeryong was by her side. It felt like a dream.

When was the last time she had looked at others around her without tension or vigilance?

When was the last time she had felt the relief of being loved and protected?

Therefore, the resonance ringing deep in her heart right now was something she was experiencing for the first time.

"Thank you so much."

Dana's trembling voice contained all her emotions.

"You may stay here while Daeryong trains."

Since Seo Daeryong trained until late after finishing his duties, it meant they could be together.

Just when Seo Daeryong was even more moved than she was, One Flower Sword Venerable abruptly spoke.

"He is a child who uses the sword; what help could there be in watching him swing around such crude blades?"

"Crude!"

"Watching that frustrating master and disciple will make my blood boil. So..."

Then she said something astonishing.

"Come see me whenever you have time."

Dana's eyes widened. The person she had admired was telling her to visit. It was One Flower Sword Venerable, someone she couldn't dare look straight at, someone who shouldn't even be in the same place as her.

"May I truly do that?"

One Flower Sword Venerable nodded.

"Thank you so much. I will definitely come."

Geom Mugeuk could tell that a new wind of fate was beginning to blow for Dana as well.

Geom Mugeuk said to Seo Daeryong,

"Today, you're buying the drinks!"

* * *

Han Seol was crossing the lake by boat.

The one rowing the boat was Yeobin, one of the Three Great Drunkards.

Han Seol had the Hanbing Twin Swords wait on the other side of the lake and boarded the boat alone. Sa Han had said it wouldn't do, but So Bing had sent her, telling her to do so.

"Why exactly are you doing this?"

Sa Han couldn't understand his wife. At first, she had been more concerned about bodyguards than even he was.

"Both the Cult Leader and the Young Cult Leader. And that Extreme Evil Lesser Demon we saw too. Did any of them have weaknesses?"

Even the demons they had seen while passing through the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had been properly disciplined.

"In the end, if someone targets the Young Palace Lady, it would definitely be with the Cult Leader's permission. Could we stop that?"

"So you're saying we should abandon protection like this?"

"It's not abandoning; it's letting the Young Palace Lady look around and play freely."

Sa Han guessed her reason for doing this.

"You trust the Young Cult Leader."

"I don't trust the Young Cult Leader."

She answered firmly, her gaze directed far away at Han Seol on the boat.

"Han Seol is no longer the little child we knew. It's the Young Palace Lady who is becoming an adult that I trust."

The boat Han Seol rode arrived at the island in the middle of the lake.

Following Yeobin's guidance, she ascended to the Drunken Dream Pavilion.

Drunken Demon was drinking alone in the Drunken Dream Pavilion.

"I greet the Demon Lord."

"Young Palace Lady, welcome! I heard you had arrived."

She had come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and while she didn't know about the other Demon Lords, she had wanted to pay her respects to Drunken Demon since he was the one who had rescued the North Sea Ice Palace with the Young Cult Leader.

"The lake is truly beautiful. This pavilion is beautiful as well."

"The problem is that an incorrigible drunkard lives in such a beautiful place."

At Drunken Demon's joke, Han Seol smiled.

"It suits you well, this place."

Drunken Demon sensed she had changed from when he had seen her at the Ice Palace before.

"Come, sit."

Han Seol saw the cup at Drunken Demon's waist.

Drunken Demon placed the cup before her.

"Do you know what cup this is?"

"Yes, it is the Ice Palace Holy Grail, a treasure of our cult."

"Have you ever drunk from this cup?"

When Han Seol shook her head, he poured wine into the cup.

"I've cleaned it thoroughly, so have a drink."

"I will drink gratefully."

Han Seol drank the wine in the Ice Palace Holy Grail. Though she wasn't someone who particularly enjoyed alcohol, Han Seol's eyes widened roundly.

"The wine tastes truly excellent."

"Good wine combined with a good cup."

Thus the two of them drank several cups back and forth.

After gazing at the lake for a moment, she spoke to Drunken Demon.

"I never thought I would come this far."

Drunken Demon made an expression showing he understood.

"You came to your senses and realized you're in the cult proper after being with the Young Cult Leader?"

Han Seol smiled faintly and nodded slightly. It was an exaggeration, but close enough.

"When I'm with him, I think things I've never thought of in my life."

"For example?"

After being lost in thought for a moment.

"I want to live like this person."

Drunken Demon poured wine into her empty cup and said.

"That's normal."

"Pardon?"

"I've had that thought dozens of times."

Because it was an unexpected remark, Han Seol looked at Drunken Demon with a surprised face.

"You compare yourself and feel jealous. And then you get angry and want to make excuses. But do you know? The moment you get angry and want to make excuses is precisely when you can best understand the person called you."

When you get angry and when you retort, that is where I am?

She felt like she understood and like she didn't.

Drunken Demon laughed and emptied his wine cup.

"The conclusion is, we can't live like the Young Cult Leader. Because the Young Cult Leader is crazy!"

Han Seol laughed and drank from her cup as well. 'Crazy'—she thought it was a word only Drunken Demon could say.

"Come, a toast between comrades!"

She thought it was good that she had come to see Drunken Demon. Actually, she had hesitated a little. Because of what Geom Mugeuk had said.

See much on this journey.

For one reason or another, she thought it wouldn't be easy to get away from Geom Mugeuk. She had thought that if she came here, she would only talk about Drunken Demon and herself, yet here she was talking about the Young Cult Leader again.

Still, being able to converse so naturally with Drunken Demon was possible because they had the topic of Geom Mugeuk.

"I suppose I'll drink at the Fengliu Tavern this evening."

"How did you know?"

"Because it's something related to alcohol?"

Drunken Demon handed Han Seol the entire box of wine that had been in the corner of the Drunken Dream Pavilion.

"Please convey my congratulations on the Young Cult Leader's safe return to the cult."

* * *

When Han Seol arrived at the Fengliu Tavern, no one had arrived yet.

"Welcome!"

Jo Chunbae greeted her politely. Having greeted her earlier, he knew she was the Young Palace Lady of the North Sea Ice Palace.

"This way, please."

"Before that, please take this first."

She handed over the box of wine that the Hanbing Twin Swords had carried.

"This is a gift from Drunken Demon for the Young Cult Leader."

"Yes, I will serve this wine today."

One might think he'd feel displeased about outside wine being brought in, but Jo Chunbae didn't mind at all. She didn't yet know how strong a bond Geom Mugeuk and Jo Chunbae shared.

Jo Chunbae guided her to the fixed seats on the upper floor.

The Hanbing Twin Swords didn't follow up and waited on the first floor.

Normally, Sa Han would have come up to the upper floor and taken a corner seat, but this time too, So Bing told him to just wait here. On top of that—

"Dear, let's have a drink too. We haven't properly had a drink since coming to the Central Plains."

"Let's. If the Young Palace Lady ends up coming to harm and we die anyway, we should at least have one last drink."

He spoke as if resigned, but that wasn't his true feeling.

Whenever there was a major affair, he had always trusted his wife's intuition and judgment, and they had survived well until now. He trusted his wife's judgment now too.

As Han Seol was about to sit in the upper floor seats, she was startled to see writing engraved on the wall.

"Is this really writing left by the Cult Leader and the Lords?"

Jo Chunbae answered proudly.

"Yes, it is. They left it during the Three Powers Meeting."

Not only them, but writings left by the Demon Lords as well were there.

She had never seen a tavern with such writing on the walls before. It was novel and surprising.

The words 'Young Palace Lady, please leave a word as well' hovered on Jo Chunbae's lips, but he didn't dare voice them.

Then Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong entered the tavern.

"Innkeeper, this person is buying drinks today, so treat him well."

"Why are you doing this? I have people to feed now too, you know?"

"They're people who'd eat and live fine even without you."

After them, the Dana sisters followed in. Starting tomorrow, they would enter a new organization, so today would be their last day to drink comfortably with easy hearts for the time being.

Dan-yeon took Seo Daeryong's side.

"Stop tormenting my brother-in-law!"

Startled, Dana scolded her for being rude, and Seo Daeryong beamed.

"How cruel, youngest sister-in-law! How is a man without a sister-in-law supposed to live?"

Jo Chunbae, who had come down from the upper floor, said to them,

"Don't worry about the drinks today. Lord Drunken Demon has sent wine. After a long time, I will treat you to the food myself."

Seo Daeryong's expression brightened brilliantly. He whispered to Dan-yeon as if telling a secret.

"I saved money, so I'll give my sister-in-law pocket money!"

Geom Mugeuk laughed. He had never seen Seo Daeryong so happy.

Yes, laugh like that.

The reason he had returned was to protect that smile. So laugh and enjoy yourself to your heart's content.

Then someone entered the tavern.

"Young Cult Leader!"

This time, the one who had arrived was Demon Lord Jang Ho.

"Lord Jang! Have you been well?"

"Young Cult Leader! I'm relieved you returned safely."

The two happily reunited. Jang Ho, dependable anytime one saw him, seemed to have larger muscles.

"Lord Jang!"

The one who was truly happy to see him was Seo Daeryong. He was brimming with the desire to boast about Dana the moment they had a moment alone.

Because apart from Geom Mugeuk, the only people he could boast to with a peaceful heart were Yi An and Jang Ho.

Then Geom Mugeuk saw someone peeking inside the door and then trying to pass by.

"Chief Gang!"

It was Gang Dal. Geom Mugeuk had told him to come, so he had come, but he had hesitated several times about whether to actually show up. It might have been a mere polite invitation. Even now, he had considered just passing by and going back.

"Over here, where are you going!"

Geom Mugeuk welcomed him so gladly that his tension eased.

"I'm not sure if it's a place I should come to."

"This gathering started because of you, Chief Gang! Without you, this gathering wouldn't exist in the first place."

Hearing that, Gang Dal was happy. Since coming to the main hall, he had been busy adapting and hadn't even realized he was in good spirits. But when Geom Mugeuk returned, it began to feel like living among people.

"I greet the Demon Lord!"

Gang Dal politely greeted Jang Ho. He hadn't dreamed that the Demon Lord would be at this drinking gathering.

"Pleasure."

Perhaps because Geom Mugeuk was there? The Demon Lord felt different here than when seen within the cult.

The last to arrive was Cheon Sohui, successor to the Fist Demon.

"It's been a while, Young Cult Leader."

After becoming Fist Demon's successor, she had been known to devote herself solely to martial training, so he had called her today. Not for Han Seol's sake, but for Cheon Sohui's. To see people and take a breather.

With everyone gathered at the entrance like this, Geom Mugeuk looked up at the upper floor.

"Everyone's here now."

Han Seol looked down at them with a surprised expression. When he had said there were friends in the cult he wanted to introduce, she had thought it would be one or two people at most.

But she hadn't known he would call this many people. Was he calling everyone in the cult?

Cheon Sohui's words came to mind.

'That's right, this person is crazy.'

Geom Mugeuk walked up ahead.

"Innkeeper, close the doors and lay out the tables!"

Chapter 562: Pretense Stronger Than Sincerity
---------------------------------------------
Everyone sat around the seats on this floor.

First, the Dan sisters introduced themselves.

Unlike the composed greetings of her two older sisters, the youngest, Danyeon, proudly boasted of her new organization with vigor.

"Until today I was a wanderer, but starting tomorrow, I will be Danyeon of the Gwiyeongdae!"

Han Seol formed a faint smile. Upon arriving, he had introduced the tavern keeper, and now he introduced the wanderer sisters as friends. From the successors of the Murim Alliance and the Demonic Alliance to the tavern keeper and wanderers, the one who kept them all as friends was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

Jang Ho looked at Geom Mugeuk, asking with his eyes what was going on.

He had met the Dan sisters during the fight at the Jeong'an branch earlier, but he had never expected to see them here at the main hall.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Dana and spoke.

"My right arm has finally found half a wing."

That explanation was enough.

Instead of offering congratulations to Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho politely spoke to Dana.

"May I offer you a drink?"

"Ah, yes."

Jang Ho politely poured alcohol for the flustered Dana. It was the moment a woman who had lived as a wanderer received a drink from the Demon Lord.

"Welcome, sister-in-law."

At the word sister-in-law, Dana was surprised, and Seo Daeryong was even more surprised. It meant that he regarded him as a friend, and as a brother as well.

Only after that did Jang Ho politely greet everyone. By taking special care of Dana, he was showing courtesy to Seo Daeryong.

How could Seo Daeryong not know his heart? Seo Daeryong bowed his head to Jang Ho in greeting. Thank you, my Lord.

Next, Gang Dal greeted them. He still felt awkward in this seat.

Geom Mugeuk stepped forward and formally introduced him.

"Great Lord Gang saved three young ladies here. Thanks to that, countless lives could be saved."

Gang Dal was thankful to Geom Mugeuk. In truth, that choice had ultimately been one for himself. It hadn't been grand righteousness or a spirit of sacrifice.

But Geom Mugeuk didn't disparage his choice as merely lucky, instead evaluating it as more valuable than it actually was. In a world where everyone was busy cutting others down.

Geom Mugeuk gave Gang Dal a drink.

"Accept a drink from me."

"Yes, Young Cult Leader."

"The responsibility you now bear is extremely heavy. From now on, please uphold the laws and discipline of our cult well."

"I will guard them with all my loyalty."

When the drink poured by the Young Cult Leader passed down his throat, Gang Dal finally realized he was living a new life.

After Gang Dal's greeting ended, Cheon Sohui greeted them. Cheon Sohui was the one who had rushed over immediately upon Geom Mugeuk's sudden summons. She was someone who would run anywhere if Geom Mugeuk called, even if she wouldn't do it for anyone else.

It wasn't simply because she had become a successor thanks to Geom Mugeuk. It was because when she became the Fist Demon, he was the future Cult Leader she would serve.

"She is the direct disciple of the Fist Demon, and the one who will become the next Fist Demon."

Those who hadn't known her were all startled. How could they have known the next Fist Demon would attend this drinking gathering? Moreover, that the next Fist Demon was a woman?

The Dan sisters exchanged surprised looks. From the Demon Lord to the next Fist Demon. Was it okay for them to be in a place like this? Truly, ever since arriving at the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, it had been a continuous stream of surprises.

"And she is also my only little martial sister."

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly as he looked at Cheon Sohui.

"Have you been well?"

"Yes, Elder Martial Brother."

She was supposed to call him Young Cult Leader in front of others, but because he had introduced her as his little martial sister at the end, she called him Elder Martial Brother.

No one knew how much she wanted to boast of being the Young Cult Leader's little martial sister. Today, because Geom Mugeuk had set the stage, she revealed the pride she had been holding back.

"Is Master well? I'm throwing a drinking party before even greeting him."

It wasn't as if the Fist Demon was the only one. Except for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Lord, he was in a situation where he hadn't met the other Demonic Venerables yet.

"Now then, let me introduce today's guest of honor."

Geom Mugeuk lastly introduced Han Seol.

"She is the Young Palace Lady of the North Sea Ice Palace."

Those who didn't know her were surprised again too. Since they were in the main headquarters of the Demonic Cult, one could expect to meet the next Fist Demon, but the next Ice Palace Lord?

"The purpose of today's gathering is to introduce my friends of the Central Plains to the Young Palace Lady of the Ice Palace."

At the words "my friends," both Gang Dal and the Dan sisters were inwardly nervous. Friends with the Young Cult Leader? It was an absurd notion.

"Furthermore, it is also a celebration of Great Lord Gang's advance to the main hall."

Geom Mugeuk's gaze moved from Gang Dal to the Dan sisters.

"It is also a celebration of the new lives of you three."

Next, looking at Cheon Sohui.

"It is also a place to have fun with my little martial sister after a long time."

Lastly, he looked at Jang Ho and Seo Daeryong.

"It is a place to attach my right arm and left arm to this body that has returned alive."

Geom Mugeuk raised his cup.

"Now, for today alone, let's put everything down and enjoy ourselves!"

After a vigorous toast, everyone emptied their cups. As Geom Mugeuk led the atmosphere, the drinking gathering heated up in an instant.

Geom Mugeuk filled Han Seol's cup and asked.

"What did you talk about with the Drunken Demon?"

Han Seol smiled an inscrutable smile. It was all stories about you—whose else would it be?

"We talked about alcohol, about a beautiful lake, and about a crazy person."

"A crazy person? Who do you mean?"

"There is such a person. Someone who makes me angry every time I see him."

When Han Seol stared at him, Geom Mugeuk could tell that person was himself.

"Don't believe a drunkard's words!"

At those words, Han Seol laughed.

"That's right. There probably isn't another crazy person as diligent as you."

Geom Mugeuk shook his head as if refusing to admit it.

"Do you know what I felt during this outing from the cult? I need to become more diligent. I had thought I was truly diligent, but this time I nearly died. I had forgotten for a moment."

Geom Mugeuk spoke, emptying his cup.

"That the enemy is always more diligent than me."

At the words that Geom Mugeuk had nearly lost his life, Jang Ho drank alone. It was upsetting. It was fortunate that he had returned alive like this, but if something had happened?

If only he could, he wanted to lead the demon army and assist Geom Mugeuk. It was only a shame that realistically, he could not.

Seo Daeryong silently extended his cup, and Jang Ho toasted with him. The frustration contained in Seo Daeryong's cup was similar to Jang Ho's regret.

Just as the atmosphere was slightly subsiding, Jo Chunbae came up with both hands full of dishes.

"Thank you for waiting."

"Oh, I was really scolded for wanting to eat the food our tavern keeper made."

"I've made it with specially prepared, fine ingredients, so please enjoy."

Geom Mugeuk stopped him as he tried to leave after setting down the dishes.

"Where are you going? It's been a while since we met, so you must have a drink before leaving."

"Oh my, no. How could I intrude in a gathering with such noble people?"

He waved his hands trying to step back, but was forcibly seated.

"There is no one more precious than the tavern keeper here. Now, sit and accept a drink."

"Let me pour for you first."

"Accept it."

Jo Chunbae received the drink with an expression of deep gratitude and drank it. Whether in good times or bad, this was the Geom Mugeuk who treated him without change.

Jo Chunbae was someone who had gone through all sorts of hardships while running a drinking business his whole life. He knew very well how difficult it was to treat someone without change like this. Even when there was an incomparable difference in status.

"The alcohol tastes too good."

Jo Chunbae politely poured alcohol for Geom Mugeuk.

"As expected, the alcohol given by our tavern keeper is the most delicious."

"I am truly glad you have returned safely."

"Oh, and I've set aside one bottle of alcohol, so drink it when you think of the tavern keeper's liquor."

"Oh my, it's fine."

"It's a chance to drink the alcohol bestowed by the Drunken Demon. Do not refuse."

"Thank you."

Han Seol watched Geom Mugeuk as if observing him.

Could it be that there was no pretense at all in all those actions? Was he acting now to show me? That he is such a broad-minded, inclusive person. Could he be pulling a con?

Even knowing it wasn't the case, she kept doubting. How could he be like that?

Suddenly, the Drunken Demon's words came to mind.

—Because I've thought about it dozens of times over.

Could those dozens of times have been only about wanting to live like this person? It must mean that he had doubted and doubted again whether it was pretense.

Leaning on the railing, watching Geom Mugeuk talking with Jo Chunbae until the very end as he went down to the first floor, she reached a conclusion.

Yes, it might be pretense. But that pretense is a pretense stronger than sincerity. No one's sincerity could ever keep up with that pretense.

Han Seol drank another cup.

Just then, her eyes met Dana's, who had been looking at her from the side.

Dana smiled and slightly bowed her head. It was an expression of how dare she make eye contact with the Young Palace Lady of the North Sea Ice Palace.

"Please accept a drink from me."

Han Seol poured a drink for Dana.

"You are my very first wanderer friend."

Dana was startled by those words.

"Friends? Those are undeserved words."

Han Seol knew that wasn't the case. Just looking at this gathering, that Seo Daeryong over there truly liked this woman, and Jang Ho treated her respectfully. And would that Geom Mugeuk be someone who would look down on others for being wanderers, or hold someone in high regard just for being a Young Palace Lady?

It was a place where what mattered was not what status one had, but what kind of person one was.

She looked at Geom Mugeuk. He had introduced her as the guest of honor, but the true host of this gathering was Geom Mugeuk.

That host was now talking with Cheon Sohui.

"Is it hard?"

He could tell from the burst of laughter at that question how hard it was for her. She was truly working herself to death. Because being told the successor had been wrongly chosen was something she hated more than death. And because it would be a disgrace to her master and Geom Mugeuk as well.

"I sometimes miss the late-night training sessions."

"Me too."

Come to think of it, it was the late-night training that had become the opportunity to get close to the Fist Demon.

"There will be a day when we gather again."

"Will such a day come?"

"It will definitely come."

For that day. To gather as they did today, laughing and chatting.

That was the reason Geom Mugeuk worked so hard.

Thus, the drinking gathering gradually ripened. Everyone drank with relaxed hearts.

It was something that normally couldn't happen, but for this gathering alone, it felt like there was some kind of guarantor.

If they were people invited by Geom Mugeuk?

It was because they had faith that these were people they could be relaxed with.

Then, Han Seol and Jang Ho's eyes met.

When greeting at first, he had given Dana a drink, but he hadn't yet given one to Han Seol. If it were the past, she would have felt slighted by such a trivial thing, but...

"Please accept a drink from me."

Now she offered the drink first.

"Thank you."

Jang Ho politely received and drank it.

"May I also offer you one?"

"Of course."

Jang Ho politely poured the drink. She could tell from his expression that he held no ill feelings toward her. He was someone who didn't try to look good to anyone, silently keeping his place. He had shown courtesy only for Seo Daeryong's sake.

If she hadn't offered him a drink, he would have been left with an uncomfortable feeling toward her, but simply offering a drink had changed the feeling toward a person.

If one accumulated these small things, one would become like Geom Mugeuk.

When she looked at Geom Mugeuk, he was not at his seat.

He was in an unexpected place.

At some point he had gone down and was drinking with the Hanbing Twin Swords.

Sahan and Sobing, who had said to never trust the Young Cult Leader, were laughing heartily at whatever they had heard.

Yes, the Drunken Demon's words were right.

'I can't live like you.'

She admitted it. No matter how much she accumulated, she could never become like that. Taking care of these people while also taking care of the Hanbing Twin Swords on the first floor?

Though she couldn't live like Geom Mugeuk, what she felt and learned was immense.

Enough to make her think that though she was a child when she left the North Sea, perhaps she would return as an adult.

When Geom Mugeuk returned to his seat, this time Danyeon came to his side and asked with a flushed, drunken face.

"You said it before, didn't you? That the Gwiyeongdae is the place where the Commander, the heart of the Young Cult Leader, resides."

"I did."

"What kind of person is the Commander? I'm so curious."

She had mustered courage with the help of alcohol.

Then Seo Daeryong answered instead.

"She is beautiful."

He had cut in with joy as the talk of Ian came up.

Danyeon narrowed her eyes and asked. She had asked what kind of person she was, but beautiful?

"How much so?"

It was the moment Seo Daeryong was about to excitedly say she could be called the world's greatest beauty.

Geom Mugeuk's urgent telepathic message flew in.

—Get a hold of yourself!

In an instant, Seo Daeryong's drunkenness vanished.

Seo Daeryong looked at Dana beside him and said.

"She's pretty, though not as much as Lady Dan."

Dana's face flushed slightly as she spoke.

"Please don't say unnecessary things."

Seeing her expression that wasn't displeased despite that, Seo Daeryong sent a telepathic message to Geom Mugeuk.

—I survived! Thank you!

—Letting your guard down means death!

Danyeon asked again.

"Then where is the Commander now?"

It was right when Han Seol, who had been listening beside them, was feeling that she missed her unni.

Squeak.

The tavern door opened and a person came inside.

Everyone's gaze turned there.

Jo Chunbae smiled happily upon seeing the person who entered.

The Hanbing Twin Swords looked in surprise. The person who entered was a woman, and when she stepped in, they had the illusion that the surroundings brightened.

Not only the three Dan sisters but also Gang Dal opened their eyes wide. They had never seen such a beautiful woman since birth.

Jang Ho and Seo Daeryong smiled brightly with expressions saying, she's finally here!

She was none other than Ian.

Her gaze had become even clearer and deeper, and her aura gave off a different feeling than before.

Geom Mugeuk could tell.

'She has finally achieved the Grand Completion of the Flying Heaven Sword Technique!'

The achievement of Grand Completion had bestowed upon her yet another new beauty. Now it wasn't just beauty; a strong, powerful beauty could be felt.

Ian looked up at Geom Mugeuk and spoke calmly.

"Commander of the Gwiyeongdae, Ian, has returned after completing her mission."

Her gaze looked upon Geom Mugeuk, Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho, and Han Seol.

Delight bloomed across her face. Ian smiled brightly and said.

"What, you're having this much fun without me?"

Chapter 563: I Went to Such Trouble to Bring It, So Take It
-----------------------------------------------------------
Ian slowly walked up to this floor.

Seen up close, Ian was even more beautiful than when viewed from below. Moreover, one could feel an unapproachable air about her.

“Young Cult Leader!”

Ian stood before Geom Mugeuk.

If other people hadn’t been there, she wouldn’t have called him Young Cult Leader but Young Master. “Young Master!” She would have run and embraced him tightly. She had missed Geom Mugeuk that much.

“You’ve worked hard.”

At those words from Geom Mugeuk, Ian felt her stiff, tense heart melt away like a lie.

‘You’ve finally returned!’

Ian felt exactly the same emotion that Geom Mugeuk had felt upon seeing his father.

Ian’s gaze naturally turned to Hanseol.

The first reason Hanseol had come to the Heavenly Demon Cult was to meet Ian. She had worried about leaving without seeing her, so it was fortunate that Ian had returned.

Hanseol called out to her.

“Sister.”

It was the first time they had met since parting after deciding to be sisters. She had expected it to be very awkward and distant, but unexpectedly it was not.

At the word “Sister,” everyone who didn’t know their relationship was startled.

“I didn’t expect to see you here.”

“Didn’t you say you’d show me around when I come to the Central Plains next time?”

She remembered exactly what Ian had said.

“Yes, welcome.”

Watching Ian smile at her, Hanseol felt that Ian had changed somehow. Yet she couldn’t tell exactly what kind of change it was.

Seo Daeryong gladly greeted Ian.

“This martial artist is glad you have returned safely.”

“Has the Pavilion Lord been well?”

Then Geom Mugeuk spoke up slyly.

“He’s fared the best among us.”

As he said this, he looked at Dana. The quick-witted Ian opened her eyes wide.

“No way? You traitor!”

Ian showed their intimacy with a casual joke, and Seo Daeryong laughed sheepishly.

Ian said to Dana with a bright smile.

“Congratulations.”

“Thank you, Lord.”

Dana looked at the youngest and spoke.

“The youngest was able to enter the Ghost Shadow Corps thanks to the Young Cult Leader. Hurry and greet her.”

Dan-yeon stared blankly at Ian, mesmerized, then belatedly came to her senses.

“Ah, you are so beautiful. I am Dan-yeon, who has just joined as a new recruit. The First Squad Leader told me to report starting tomorrow.”

Dan-yeon politely greeted her with cupped fists.

Ian quietly gazed at her, then suddenly asked a question.

“Have you heard what kind of organization we are?”

“Yes, I heard it is a place that becomes the Young Cult Leader’s shadow.”

“Good, then I’ll ask. If the Cult Leader’s command and the Young Cult Leader’s command conflict, what will you do?”

Dan-yeon answered without hesitation.

“I should follow the Young Cult Leader’s command, shouldn’t I?”

“Then wouldn’t that be treason?”

She flinched at the word treason, but Dan-yeon’s thoughts were firm.

“A shadow is destined to disappear without the body, after all.”

At Dan-yeon’s answer, Ian looked at Geom Mugeuk and spoke.

“She hasn’t even started her first day yet, but her loyalty is greater than mine?”

At those words, a smile formed on Dan-yeon’s face. She liked interesting people. Indeed, she was the brightest and wittiest among the three sisters. The earlier joke calling Seo Daeryong a traitor, and now too—she liked Ian very much.

The great goodwill felt toward Geom Mugeuk was not solely because he was the Young Cult Leader. It was because of the relaxed jokes he threw out at times like this.

“But you’ll still send the food to Father, won’t you?”

At Ian’s questioning gaze, Geom Mugeuk merely smiled an inscrutable smile.

Jang Ho greeted her once more.

“This martial artist is glad you have returned safely.”

“My Lord has grown even more imposing!”

The reunion with Cheon Sohee was also after a long time.

“We used to see each other every day, but now it’s hard to see your face even once.”

“The Young Cult Leader said he would definitely revive the midnight training.”

After finally greeting Gangdal as well, they sat down together.

Geom Mugeuk raised his glass high and shouted.

“My heart has returned!”

Ian was embarrassed, thinking, He’s doing this again, but still raised her glass high.

And so everyone toasted cheerfully and drank.

Setting down her glass, Dan-yeon whispered to Seo Daeryong.

“Brother-in-law, that was too obvious a lie.”

She was referring to what Seo Daeryong had said earlier, that his sister was prettier.

As Seo Daeryong laughed awkwardly, Dan-yeon whispered again.

“At times like this, you say, ‘In my eyes, you’re prettier.’”

“Ah!”

Seo Daeryong also whispered to Dan-yeon.

“Please teach me well from now on, sister-in-law.”

“Won’t the quality of advice depend on the size of my brother-in-law’s allowance?”

At those words, Seo Daeryong laughed. A thought suddenly came to his mind.

‘Am I allowed to be this happy?’

He feared that Heaven might be jealous of this happiness and send misfortune.

Then he suddenly looked at Geom Mugeuk drinking with Ian.

He felt he now knew for certain in his heart what he had only vaguely understood in his head. Why Geom Mugeuk had moved so diligently.

To Geom Mugeuk, Ian must be Dana, Jang Ho must be Danbi, and he himself must be Dan-yeon. He had moved so diligently to ensure that misfortune would not dare approach. To ensure that laziness did not invite misfortune.

‘I too will protect it through diligence, Young Cult Leader.’

Just then, their eyes met, and the two of them toasted in the air before emptying their cups.

Even though there were people meeting for the first time, the atmosphere was pleasant.

The pillar that was Geom Mugeuk put everyone at ease.

Laughing and chatting, everyone conversed comfortably.

Toward the end of the drinking gathering, they broke into smaller groups to talk.

Danbi, who was to enter the Yellow Heaven Pavilion as an executioner, conversed with Seo Daeryong and Gangdal. She was usually the second daughter who didn’t speak much, but she wasn’t so introverted as to miss an opportunity to build rapport with her direct superiors.

Seo Daeryong spoke to his two subordinates.

“I think our Yellow Heaven Pavilion is a guiding star. Its role is to keep one from losing their way on a dark night. Because everyone tries to find an easier path, even if every organization in the cult is shaken, we alone must not be shaken.”

Danbi asked cautiously.

“How can we not be shaken?”

Then Seo Daeryong answered honestly.

“Each person must find their own guiding star.”

Gangdal and Danbi fell into thought and quietly emptied their cups.

Seo Daeryong’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. He could say this because he had already found his.

Meanwhile, Dan-yeon was looking only at Ian.

She had completely fallen for Ian, the leader of the organization she belonged to. Beautiful, strong, and above all, a witty person. Ian was truly the very image she dreamed of becoming.

“Lord, do you have a dream?”

Ian answered Dan-yeon’s courageous question.

“To survive well for a long time.”

Ian left out one important word.

Geom Mugeuk drank and talked with Cheon Sohee and Jang Ho about this and that. They spoke of the Fist Demon, and of Jang Ho’s painting.

Cheon Sohee felt like she could finally breathe freely after so long. Gathering like this to talk and drink was wonderful. She used to drink comfortably with the iron fists of the Donggwon Sect, but after becoming the successor, such occasions had disappeared.

“We’ll take our leave first.”

Since they had to face their first day at new organizations the next day, the Dan sisters rose from their seats first.

Dana had been quiet throughout the drinking gathering.

In truth, she was someone who drank well and could talk a great deal if she tried, but today she refrained from both drinking and speaking. To avoid any mistakes for Seo Daeryong and her younger sisters.

Before parting, Ian spoke softly to Dana.

“Pavilion Lord Seo is truly a good person.”

“Thank you.”

As they rose from their seats, Gangdal also bid farewell.

“A subordinate goes to prepare for tomorrow; can the leader afford to be idle?”

He said that, but it was because he felt it was time to step aside for their long-awaited reunion. He was one who knew when to join in and when to step away decisively.

Hanseol also stood up.

“The elders seem tired, so I should be going too.”

On the first floor, Sahan of the Hanbing Twin Swords was asleep with his head on his wife’s shoulder. He was drunk from drinking after a long time.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Hanseol.

“Miss Han, I have a request.”

“Please speak.”

“Please leave a word on this wall.”

At those words, Jo Chunbae’s face on the first floor brightened. As expected, Geom Mugeuk understood and looked after her wishes.

She immediately shook her head.

“I don’t think I’m qualified.”

“Then leave it on this wall. Here we will leave the names of our young experts. Let our Young Palace Lord open the beginning.”

With that request, Hanseol eventually left a message.

—Drank well with friends and now leaving. Hanseol, Young Palace Lord of the Northern Sea Ice Palace.

It was the moment the successor of the Northern Sea Ice Palace finally left writing on the wall of the Pungnyu Tavern.

At the expression “friends,” Seo Daeryong smiled warmly, and Jo Chunbae beamed with joy.

“Thank you. Truly, thank you.”

Jo Chunbae expressed gratitude to Hanseol, then looked at Geom Mugeuk and smiled broadly. She would never have left those words if not for Geom Mugeuk.

And so everyone left, and only Geom Mugeuk, Cheon Sohee, and the drinking trio remained at the Pungnyu Tavern. Geom Mugeuk had deliberately kept Cheon Sohee from leaving though she had tried to go.

“Drink for a moment. I have something to bring.”

While Geom Mugeuk briefly stepped away, the four of them chatted about this and that.

Then Ian asked Seo Daeryong.

“How does it feel to have someone you love?”

To Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho and Ian were more than simple drinking friends.

“Honestly, I returned with Miss Dan, but I’m scared. I’ve lived selfishly thinking only of myself my whole life, so I wonder if I can do well. I worry I’ll regret it later.”

Since they were all unmarried, they were no different from Seo Daeryong in sentiment.

Then Geom Mugeuk’s voice was heard from the first floor.

“Such worries should be asked of an experienced person.”

Geom Mugeuk came up to this floor bringing Jo Chunbae with him. Having gone somewhere in the meantime, he held a sword belt and a long sword case.

“Proprietor, do you regret marriage?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Jo Chunbae smiled and said.

“There were times I regretted it. After big fights with the wife, whew, I’d fall asleep dreaming of the days I lived alone.”

Everyone laughed at his honest confession.

“But I would have regretted living alone too. No matter how disobedient the brat is, I’d be terrified imagining my life without him. Whether it’s love or hate, as I get older, I have only my wife.”

Geom Mugeuk said to Seo Daeryong.

“When love troubles weigh on you, come to the Pungnyu Tavern for advice!”

“Yes!”

Jo Chunbae bowed, saying he was unworthy of such honor, then went down to the first floor. He was smiling as he descended the stairs. To think he would now end up giving love advice to the Yellow Heaven Pavilion Lord.

Seo Daeryong’s gaze turned to the case and sword belt Geom Mugeuk had brought.

“But what is that? It looks like a sword case?”

Geom Mugeuk gave the sword case to Ian.

“Here, a gift.”

Ian, receiving the case, was startled. She had never imagined receiving a gift at this gathering.

“A gift?”

The case was engraved with a sun and moon in a magnificent manner; just by looking at the exquisitely crafted case, one could tell what was inside was no ordinary item.

Inside was a single sword.

Ian slowly drew the sword.

“Ah!”

An exclamation of admiration burst from her.

Shiiiiiiing.

A chilling killing intent spread in all directions in an instant. Truly, it was no ordinary treasured sword.

“It is the Sun-Moon Sword.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, not only Ian but Jang Ho, Seo Daeryong, and Cheon Sohee were all startled. The Sun-Moon Sword was a famous treasured sword in the martial world.

It was the very Sun-Moon Sword received from the Black Serpent Commander while resolving a matter involving the Bisa in the past. The Bisa had given the sword back to Geom Mugeuk, and at that time Geom Mugeuk had said he would give it to his heart. He had kept it safe and well since then, and now the moment to give it to Ian had come.

“Why are you giving me such a precious sword?”

“Because you’ve now reached the realm where you can protect this sword. Congratulations, Ian.”

“Ah, as I thought! You knew.”

She had achieved the grand completion of the Soaring Sword Technique she had dreamed of. It was a feat impossible in the time given even for one talented in martial arts, and it was possible thanks to Geom Mugeuk, who had taught her generously without reserve.

“Are you truly giving this to me?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. To a martial artist, a sword is like life; the Sun-Moon Sword would become another life for her.

And the sword suited her well.

"I won't refuse. I know I'll never obtain a sword like this again in my life. I'll take it."

"I had a feeling you'd like it, so I even brought the sword case."

"Thank you very much, young master."

It was a burden, truly a burden, but he was so happy he felt as though he might take flight. He wanted to hurry and display the Bicheon Sword Technique with this sword.

On the other hand, receiving such a gift alone felt so burdensome that he was sorry to the other three.

How could he not know that feeling? Seo Daeryong said to Geom Mugeuk.

"No matter that I'm the heart, this is too much! What about us?"

He had deliberately played the clown, but unexpected words came from Geom Mugeuk.

"Lord Jang and you lot have yours too."

Surely not. But Geom Mugeuk began pulling things out from his leather bag.

Protective armors emerging from the leather bag.

Geom Mugeuk gave Seo Daeryong the Biho Steel Armor, Jang Ho the Geumgang Divine Armor, and Cheon Sohui the Hwayang Armor.

These armors had come from the treasure vault of the Eunha Trading Company Lord. They were exactly the ones Geom Mugeuk had worn in four layers and packed away.

"Something this precious! I cannot accept it!"

At Seo Daeryong's refusal, Geom Mugeuk put the Biho Steel Armor away again.

"Really? Then I suppose I should give this to Ian too."

As he tried to take it back, Seo Daeryong desperately refused to let go of the Biho Steel Armor.

"You can't turn this martial artist into a greedy person."

All three were deeply moved. The armors they had just received were priceless; they could never buy them even if they saved their entire lives, and even if they had the money, they could not obtain them.

Cheon Sohui vowed in her heart.

‘Someday, wearing this armor, I will definitely protect the Cult Leader.’

Jang Ho wore a troubled expression. He had already become a Demon Lord thanks to Geom Mugeuk, and now he had received such a precious gift.

"I don't know if I should accept something so precious."

"You must accept it. I became the very incarnation of greed in front of my father and packed these away desperately in order to give them to you."

"What about the Young Cult Leader's?"

At that, Geom Mugeuk spread open his clothes. The garment he wore underneath was the Black Dragon Leather Armor.

"Naturally, I took the best one, so you don't need to feel burdened."

He had deliberately worn it, afraid they might refuse.

This consideration was more precious than the armor itself.

"Thank you very much, Young Cult Leader."

Geom Mugeuk looked at them with a pleased heart.

Yes, become stronger. Become so strong that no one would dare touch us.

"This is not a gift for you. It is a gift to my heart, my right arm, my left arm, and my only fourth sibling, so it is a gift I give to myself."

* * *

After the drinking party ended, Geom Mugeuk and Ian walked along a mountain trail at dawn. Ian brought up something he hadn't said while others were around.

"Aren't you going to ask about this mission?"

The mission was to bring Seo Jin, the Ghost Spirit Lord's younger sibling, to serve as the leader of the Second Squad of the Ghost Shadow Corps.

Seo Jin.

The only friend in his life before the regression.

If he had failed to meet her, his regression would never have come to pass.

In this life, he hoped she would form a bond with Ian rather than remaining a wanderer, and live a happier life.

"You failed, didn't you?"

If he had succeeded, he would have returned with her.

"Did something else happen this time too?"

Previously, Ian had become entangled in another matter through the fateful connection of receiving water from a woman.

"No, this time I met the young lady Seo."

"You met her?"

"I even managed to persuade her. It was really not easy."

"Then why didn't she come with you?"

"She said she would come after finishing what she had to do. She said she had business at the Hwangryong Martial Academy."

Hwangryong Martial Academy.

It was a massive martial arts academy that had produced countless masters, truly the greatest in the martial world in both name and reality.

"She said she'd come straightaway after finishing her business... Young Cult Leader?"

Ian turned his head back. Geom Mugeuk had stopped walking with a stiff expression.

"What's wrong?"

Because in his previous life, the one who had killed her was someone from the Hwangryong Martial Academy. Most likely, that ill-fated connection had begun around this time. Or perhaps fate had changed.

Geom Mugeuk could feel it. The sweet rest of the past few days had ended, and now was the moment he had to step back out into the martial world.

"Ian, you haven't unpacked yet, have you?"

Chapter 564: I Gave It for Your Sake
------------------------------------
“What on earth is going on?”

At Ian’s question, Geom Mugeuk pondered how to answer.

“I once heard information that there were problems inside the Yellow Dragon Martial Hall. I was worried it might have a bad influence on Miss Seo.”

It would be hard to understand him going straight to find her just because he had such information. The words that would make Ian understand were rather these.

“I have a bad feeling.”

Sure enough, Ian immediately nodded.

“Then we should go at once.”

How could all the many things Geom Mugeuk had done until now be understood? Ian understood everything incomprehensible about Geom Mugeuk by accepting that he was a man sent by the heavens.

“You haven’t even had a chance to rest since coming back. I’m sorry.”

“Why are you sorry? Miss Seo’s affairs are my affairs. But will you be going with me, Young Cult Leader?”

“Of course.”

At the words that he would go with her, Ian’s face brightened radiantly. Leaving the cult with Geom Mugeuk was truly what she wished for.

“Are we leaving right now?”

“The sun will rise before long. Let’s meet Miss Han, talk to her, and then depart.”

“Then I’ll be able to see my father before I go.”

For her entire life, there had only ever been one Young Cult Leader in it, but now she had gained a father and a younger sister as well.

The two returned to the main headquarters and parted ways for their respective quarters.

“Get even a sijin or two of sleep. I’ll see you later.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk was about to turn away when Ian spoke.

“Thank you for the Sun-Moon Sword. I like it so much I don’t think I’ll be able to sleep for a while.”

Looking at the sword at her waist, Geom Mugeuk said,

“It suits you well.”

He was not merely saying it. On the beautiful Ian, the Sun-Moon Sword embroidered with sun and moon suited her beyond measure.

After returning to her residence, Ian packed her things.

A change of clothes, Golden Wound Medicine, medicine for internal injuries. And she newly packed the items needed for travel into a leather pouch.

She carefully stored the sword box in her quarters. Every time she saw the box, she would be able to recall Geom Mugeuk’s heart in having so meticulously prepared even the box for her.

Her heart was too excited for sleep to come.

After driving the drunkenness out of her body with internal energy, Ian went out into the yard.

Taking a deep breath, she slowly drew the Sun-Moon Sword.

Shrrrng.

The smoothly drawn sword revealed itself in the darkness.

The dawn light, where light and darkness coexisted, flowed along the blade. The blade was so sharp that it seemed it might draw in anyone who looked at it and sever their soul.

Ian slowly roused her internal energy.

She had heard from Geom Mugeuk countless times that the moment one first obtained a treasured sword was the most important. One had to commune with the sword. For there was clearly a great difference in power between a sword that accepted its master and one that did not.

Wung.

The Sun-Moon Sword, infused with true qi, vibrated lightly. Ian strove to become one with the sword. The result of this effort would be known when a fight broke out.

‘Sun-Moon, we’re going to be together for life.’

* * *

As soon as day broke, Geom Mugeuk went with Ian to find Han Seol.

“What is it?”

She was surprised by the two of them visiting. The two had come to find her early in the morning, before she had even washed her face.

“Something suddenly came up, so I think I’ll have to leave the cult with Ian.”

At that moment, what brushed through Han Seol’s heart was unmistakably regret. Of course, Han Seol did not reveal that emotion.

“I see. If the two of you are leaving, then we should leave as well.”

Han Seol immediately informed the Frost Twin Swords that they would be departing soon.

Geom Mugeuk could not tell her to stay and enjoy herself longer before leaving. She had come to see him and Ian, after all. Seo Daeryong or Jang Ho could look after her, but she would not insist on staying to that extent.

“I’m sorry.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s apology, Han Seol looked at Ian.

“I came to see my sister. I saw her, so that’s enough.”

This time, Ian apologized to her.

“I’m sorry. I promised I’d show you around the Central Plains.”

“Next time. And the Young Cult Leader showed me a lot on the way here.”

“Are you going straight back to the Northern Sea?”

“No. There’s somewhere I need to stop by.”

Han Seol was thinking of going to meet Bi Sain before returning.

“You’ll probably see plenty of good sights there.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s meaningful words, Ian wore an expression asking what he meant, while Han Seol pretended not to hear.

Geom Mugeuk gave the two of them some time.

“I’ll go to Father first. Let’s meet at the Heavenly Demon Hall in a little while.”

Even if he was leaving, he had to bid farewell to the Cult Leader before he went.

Geom Mugeuk left the room first.

“The Young Cult Leader is always busy.”

At Han Seol’s words, Ian looked out the window at Geom Mugeuk’s figure flying far beyond the wall.

“There’s time for a cup of tea, right?”

At Han Seol’s question, Ian nodded.

“I’ll brew it for you.”

Ian personally brewed tea for her younger sister.

“The Palace Lord is well, isn’t she?”

“She’s well.”

“It’s a wonder she let you come to the Central Plains.”

“She sent me because she trusted the Young Cult Leader.”

It was true. If not for her connection with Geom Mugeuk, she would never have sent her daughter to the Central Plains.

“Sister, you seem different from when I saw you last time.”

Turning to Han Seol, Ian said with a smile,

“I went through some hardship.”

At that, Han Seol recalled the conversation she had shared with Geom Mugeuk. When she had asked if it was all right to send Ian off alone when he called her his heart, Geom Mugeuk had answered like this.

—She must know how to walk alone as well.

Han Seol thought that perhaps she was seeing now the result of that walking alone.

Meanwhile, Ian brought the tea.

The two sat facing each other and drank.

“The fragrance is nice.”

“The taste too.”

Just as Ian was savoring the scent, Han Seol abruptly asked,

“You love the Young Cult Leader, don’t you?”

It was a sudden question, but Ian was not surprised at all. Since Geom Mugeuk called her his heart every time, it would be stranger if such a question were not asked.

“I like him.”

Han Seol silently gazed at Ian.

“Why? Are you worried I’ll get hurt by a broken heart?”

Ian said, sipping her tea.

“My life is already full enough with wounds from blades.”

Ian looked out the window, and Han Seol looked with her.

Beyond the wall, a single bird was flying freely through the bright sky.

“See plenty of the Central Plains before you go.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk arrived at his father’s quarters.

Even if he left without seeing the other Demon Venerables, he had to pay his respects to his father before going.

His father was in the midst of morning training. As he waited outside the training ground, Hwi’s voice was heard.

“He says to come in.”

Geom Mugeuk stepped into the training ground.

His father stood with the Heavenly Demon Sword held out before him, eyes closed. He revealed no aura, nor did he infuse true qi into the sword. He simply stood there, feeling the sword. Last time, his father had taught him to fully feel the sword’s energy.

Geom Mugeuk also drew the Black Demon Sword. Likewise, without infusing true qi into the sword or releasing any aura, he focused only on the Black Demon Sword.

‘You’ve been busy too, haven’t you?’

It had been busy killing enemies, and busy while he trained in the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Because of that, he had not had much leisure to close his eyes like this and focus solely on the sword’s energy. Like repeatedly thinking he should look at the sky, he should look, only to keep forgetting.

As he closed his eyes and focused on the sword, he suddenly thought of his childhood.

His childhood.

It was the day he first drew not a wooden sword, but a real blade after learning swordsmanship.

The blade held in his young hand had been terribly frightening, but Geom Mugeuk pretended not to be afraid. Instead, he wore a confident expression. He vividly remembered how even at that young age, he had wanted to look good in his father’s eyes.

He recalled the words his father had spoken then.

—You must know how to fear the sword.

Thinking back, one might expect his father to tell him not to fear the sword. In any case, at the time, he had let the words pass without much thought.

‘Do I fear the sword now?’

Geom Mugeuk slowly opened his eyes. His father had, at some point, opened his eyes and was looking at him.

“Now that I think about it, lately I’ve been focusing only on the demons of the Guhwa Demonic Art. On the power of the Guhwa Demonic Art, on my accomplishments in the Guhwa Demonic Art. When will I be able to see the Heavenly Demon Soul? My mind was occupied only with such thoughts.”

With a deepened gaze, Geom Mugeuk added,

“I had completely forgotten that the sword is something frightening.”

Had he not been missing the fact that this fear was the very essence of swordsmanship?

“Even though those were the words you said to me that day, Father.”

A strange light flashed through Geom Ujin’s eyes as he looked at his son.

What about his father? What kind of conversation had his father truly been having with the Heavenly Demon Sword?

Geom Mugeuk looked down for a moment at the Black Demon Sword he held, then returned it to its sheath.

“What brings you here at this hour?”

“Something came up that requires me to leave the cult again, so I came to pay my respects.”

Looking at his father, Geom Mugeuk asked casually,

“Now that your son is going out again, you’re disappointed, aren’t you?”

The corners of his father’s mouth rose slightly, but he did not deny his son’s words.

“Something urgent came up that Ian and I must handle. If a problem arises, I’ll send word through Tongcheon Pavilion.”

Geom Ujin nodded.

“Miss Han and Ian will come to the Heavenly Demon Hall to bid you farewell. Then I’ll go ahead and wait there as well.”

Geom Mugeuk was turning to leave when Geom Ujin spoke from behind him.

“I still fear the sword.”

Geom Mugeuk looked back at his father. Their gazes intertwined in the air. In the sincerity with which he spoke honestly for his son’s sake was contained the ultimate essence of swordsmanship, known only to those who had reached such a realm.

“Perhaps that is why I respect your sword, Father.”

Because he knew it was not a cruel demonic sword. Because though it was a sword stronger than any other, it was a sword that guarded against that very strength.

“Starting today, I will fear it again as well.”

* * *

Ian, Han Seol, and the Frost Twin Swords bid their farewells in the Heavenly Demon Hall.

Han Seol cupped her fist respectfully and greeted Geom Ujin.

“Thanks to you, I stayed well before leaving.”

“Return safely. Give my regards to the Palace Lord.”

“I will. May you remain in good health until we meet again.”

Han Seol bowed, and the Frost Twin Swords also bowed.

“We will see you again next time, Cult Leader.”

“You two should return safely as well.”

Next, Ian respectfully bowed with a trembling heart.

“Thank you for taking such good care of my younger sister. Then I will go and attend the Young Cult Leader well.”

Since she, too, was of the bloodline of the Northern Sea Ice Palace, she had come along to the place where Han Seol was paying her respects.

At that moment, Geom Ujin said to Han Seol and the Frost Twin Swords,

“Could you give us a moment?”

“Yes, please take your time.”

Geom Mugeuk said to Han Seol,

“Please wait in front of the Heavenly Demon Hall.”

Then Geom Ujin said to Geom Mugeuk,

“You too!”

“Me too?”

It meant everyone was to leave except Ian.

At that, Ian looked at Geom Mugeuk with a startled expression. Don’t leave me here alone!

“Ian, let’s both survive and meet again!”

Don’t leave me alone! That was Ian’s cry in her heart.

And so the four of them left the Heavenly Demon Hall.

Left alone, Ian lowered her head with a trembling heart.

‘What on earth is this about? Is there something I’m going to be scolded for? Or is he going to tell me something related to the Young Cult Leader?’

Just as her heart pounded and all sorts of thoughts filled her mind, unexpected words flowed from Geom Ujin.

“You have achieved great completion in the Flying Heaven Sword Art.”

When Ian had first entered the Heavenly Demon Hall, Geom Ujin had recognized her accomplishment. It was no other martial art but the Flying Heaven Sword Art, so there was no way he could fail to know.

“Yes, I achieved it recently. All this time, the Young Cult Leader bestowed great teachings upon this foolish one. All of it was thanks to you, Cult Leader, for allowing me to learn in the first place.”

Ian respectfully lowered her head.

“If you did not possess innate martial talent, then even if I had taught you personally rather than Mugeuk, you could not have achieved great completion in this period of time.”

At that moment, Hwi came out carrying a box and handed it to Ian.

“It is a gift from the Cult Leader.”

With a trembling heart, Ian opened the box, and to her astonishment, what lay inside was a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.

When Ian raised her head in shock, Geom Ujin spoke calmly.

“It is given in commemoration of your achieving great completion in the Flying Heaven Sword Art.”

For an instant, Ian was utterly startled. How could she ever have imagined that the Heavenly Demon would give her such a gift?

She knelt on the spot and said loudly,

“I cannot accept such a precious gift.”

Her voice trembled.

Then came a single sentence that could not be refused.

“Take it.”

Ian raised her head again to look at Geom Ujin, then soon lowered it and answered.

“Yes, Cult Leader.”

That was not all.

“Consume it now. I will watch over you.”

Ian was dazed. With trembling hands, she consumed the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng. Who else knew what it felt like to chew a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng in front of the Heavenly Demon? It was truly an indescribably astonishing experience.

When she had finished consuming the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, Geom Ujin infused a strand of internal energy into her back, helping her dissolve its power. Who could possibly have any difficulty when he was the one assisting?

The majestic internal power of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng was added to Ian’s dantian. She had recently taken the Ice Snow Herb given by the Ice Palace Lord and greatly increased her internal power, and now the internal power of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng was added on top of that.

She had truly become the possessor of internal power that would not be pushed back no matter whom she fought.

Great completion of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Sun-Moon Sword, and now even the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.

She had indeed become properly strong.

Ian respectfully bowed her head in thanks. Even if he was the Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, how could he be giving out Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng because it was common?

“I will never forget this grace until the day I die. I will repay this grace with loyalty to the Young Cult Leader.”

She took it to mean that he wanted her to protect the Young Cult Leader well.

But that was not the reason Geom Ujin had bestowed the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.

“I did not give it to you because of Mugeuk.”

“Pardon?”

“I gave it for your sake.”

Ian looked up at Geom Ujin with a startled face.

‘Even allowing me to learn the Flying Heaven Sword Art, which is originally passed down only to blood relatives, must have been because of the Young Cult Leader’s request.’

At this moment, there was a reason she had never imagined at all. It was not because she was the Young Cult Leader’s guard, nor because she was of the Ice Palace’s bloodline.

The reason was one person he cherished.

“The Fist Demon’s daughter is no different from my own daughter.”

“!”

In a gentle tone, Geom Ujin said to her,

“Go and return safely.”

Chapter 565: Take My Daughter and Come Back Safely
--------------------------------------------------
You are no different from my daughter.

The moment she heard those words, Ian was greatly surprised and flustered. She had never dreamed of hearing such words from the Cult Leader of all people. She couldn't even imagine that Cheonma viewed her in such a way.

Ian stared blankly at Geom Ujin before soon bowing her head. Her mind went blank, unsure of what to say.

"Thank you. Truly, thank you."

The only words she could offer were humble words of gratitude.

After offering her respects and walking out on the red carpet, her legs lost strength and she felt dizzy.

'I was merely a worthless bodyguard.'

And yet, the side effects of the Whole Body Petrification Art had been removed, she had inherited the Soaring Sword Technique, become the adopted daughter of the Fist Demon, and learned of her own bloodline. And today, she had heard the Cult Leader say she was no different from his daughter.

The person who had made all of that possible was waiting for her at the entrance to the Cheonma Hall.

'The Young Lord.'

What could he be saying so earnestly to the Hanbing Twin Swords?

As she approached, Geom Muguk's voice could be heard.

"...This noodle shop here is truly delicious. They say it used to be a dumpling shop. And if you go thirty li east from here, there is an inn. It may look shabby on the outside, but their boiled pork dish is outstanding. And if you go straight north from there..."

He was telling them about delicious places to eat along the way. He knew the roads well, and his memory was so good that he knew exactly what each establishment was famous for.

Of the Hanbing Twin Swords, Sahan stood back a step as if uninterested but was listening to Geom Muguk's words, while Sobing was listening eagerly.

"The places I've told you about so far should all suit your tastes, so please be sure to stop by."

Sahan glanced at Hanseol and spoke.

"It should suit the Young Lady's tastes more than ours."

Geom Muguk's gaze also turned to Hanseol.
